  reason: 'Fix the route to older withdrawals: in the prototype, "Stock y entradas"
    → "Ver todas" opens TodasLasEntradas, the business-wide entries list that must
    stay entries only, not VerTodas (prices). Name a per-variety movements list so
    withdrawals past the last few can be reached, edited and deleted.'
- pair: UI Lead→UI Designer
  category: definition
  reason: The edit form and the withdrawal detail must hold every line of a multi-variety
    withdrawal, not just one. The delete confirmation must use the product's "Antes
    de borrar" pattern with "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo", naming the reason, date and
    total grams.
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:results-buckets
  category: plan-invariant
  reason: 'Fix `_add_sale_line` in `period_figures.py:263`/`:273`: both branch on
    `DrawKind` with `if`/`else`, which breaks invariant 1. Everything else in the
    brief was checked and holds, and the seam tests pass.'
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:results-windows
  category: spec-missing-test
  reason: 'Fix first: the "withdrawal as the only record starts the history" test
    passes a stock entry on the same day, so it would pass even if withdrawals stopped
    moving the start of the history. The domain changes themselves match the brief
    and the invariants.'
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:front-withdrawal-form
  category: standard-seams
  reason: 'Move the new contract-sweep test in web/src/api/refusals.test.ts:71 to
    a declared seam (or drop it): it guards refusals.ts against contract.json but
    sits at none of the three declared screen seams. Everything else holds: the 38
    seam tests pass, the wire types match their fixtures, and no invariant is broken.'
- pair: Tech Lead→Developer:integration
  category: spec-behaviour
  reason: 'Fix first: Inicio shows Promoción and Consumo propio on Hoy when the API
    answers a withdrawal for today. Then add the PRD''s end-to-end withdrawal run,
    which is missing.'
- pair: PM→Tech Lead
  category: spec-behaviour
  reason: 'UI Lead found blocking gaps: the new screens and withdrawal states have
    no design comparisons. First register the form, the detail and the movements list
    in screens.json, and seed withdrawals in the sample app.'
escalated: null
escalation_class: null
questions_for_human: []
proposed_invariants:
- Every `match` or branch over `DrawKind` is exhaustive with no default branch, so
  a new kind cannot fall into `shortfall` or `sold`.
- A withdrawal draw is identified by an explicit source or reason field on `Draw`,
  never by the absence of `sale_id` or of a pricing rule.
- No profit, cost or pending figure is summed outside `app/results/domain/period_figures.py`.
- Every new table carries a non-null `business_id`, a declared business-removal label
  if it has no `name`, and §11 names for its date, instant and indexes.
- No module under `app/sales` or `app/payments` imports a withdrawals module.
- '`first_record` is computed only inside `scan` in `period_figures.py`, with no per-source
  port method that duplicates a date `scan` already visits.'
- Every read that feeds `allocate` or `stock_level` gets its lots and draws from the
  one assembly function, never from a direct call to `lots_and_draws` with its own
  entry and draw fetches.
- Replacing a withdrawal's lines runs the delete before the inserts, so the `(stock_withdrawal_id,
  position)` constraint cannot fail on an edit.
- A use case that edits or deletes a withdrawal passes the after-commit upkeep the
  union of the varieties of its lines before and after the change.
- A withdrawal's `reason` is stored and read only as a `WithdrawalReason` value, and
  the column has a `CheckConstraint` listing exactly those values.
- Every withdrawal write route goes through `stock_router` with `schedule_upkeep`,
  and no use case reports varieties to upkeep.
- '`draw_source_of` is called only from `lots_and_draws`, and a withdrawal''s `reason`
  is stored and read only as a `WithdrawalReason` value, checked by `ck_stock_withdrawal_reason`.'
- The future-date check and `recorded_at` of a withdrawal both come from the one injected
  clock, in the business's day.
discard_reason: null
branch: agentic/GH-83
pull_request: null
subtasks:
  stock-draw-contract:
    id: stock-draw-contract
    title: 'Stock domain: DrawSource, withdrawal draws in lot order, days left'
    brief: "Build the draw contract recorded in docs/adr/0030 (do not rewrite the\
      \ ADR; match it).\n\nFiles: new `app/stock/domain/draw_source.py`; `app/stock/domain/lot_order.py`;\
      \ `app/stock/domain/stock_level.py`; `app/results/domain/ledger.py`; the five\
      \ current callers of `lots_and_draws` (only to keep them compiling).\n\n- `draw_source.py`:\
      \ `DrawSource` enum (`ADJUSTMENT`, `SALE_LINE`, `PROMOTION`, `OWN_USE`); `WithdrawalReason`\
      \ enum with values `\"promotion\"` and `\"own_use\"`; `draw_source_of(reason:\
      \ WithdrawalReason) -> DrawSource`, an exhaustive `match` ending in `assert_never`.\n\
      - `lot_order.py`:\n  - `Draw` gains a required `source: DrawSource`. Negative\
      \ adjustments get `ADJUSTMENT`, sale lines `SALE_LINE`.\n  - Add `WITHDRAWAL_LINE_RANK\
      \ = 2`. `STOCK_ENTRY_RANK = 0` and `SALE_LINE_RANK = 1` keep their values.\n\
      \  - Add the frozen dataclass `WithdrawalLineDraw(variety_id, day, recorded_at,\
      \ withdrawal_id, position, grams, reason: WithdrawalReason)`.\n  - `lots_and_draws(entries,\
      \ sale_draws, withdrawal_draws)` takes the third argument with no default. It\
      \ turns each withdrawal line into `Draw(LotOrder(day, recorded_at, WITHDRAWAL_LINE_RANK,\
      \ withdrawal_id, position), variety_id, grams, source=draw_source_of(reason))`.\
      \ Note that `draw_source_of` is called only here.\n  - Update the module docstring.\n\
      - `stock_level._days_left`: count draws through an exhaustive `match draw.source`.\
      \ `SALE_LINE`, `PROMOTION` and `OWN_USE` count; `ADJUSTMENT` does not. Read\
      \ nothing from `rank` any more.\n- `results/domain/ledger.py`: `DrawKind = Literal[\"\
      sold\", \"gifted\", \"shortfall\", \"promotion\", \"own_use\"]`. Change nothing\
      \ else in results; `results-buckets` owns the rest.\n- The five callers (`read_stock`\
      \ ×2, `line_costs.costs_for_sale`, `SqlDrawCosts.rows`, `SqlStockFigures.read`)\
      \ pass `withdrawal_draws=[]` for now. `stock-history` replaces them. Do not\
      \ touch `draw_rows.py`.\n\nTests (pure domain, no infrastructure):\n- An exact\
      \ tie of `day` and `recorded_at` across a negative adjustment, a sale line and\
      \ two withdrawal lines orders them adjustment → sale line → withdrawal lines\
      \ by position. `allocate` gives the first lot's cost to the expected draw.\n\
      - A withdrawal past every lot is pending grams, never costed $0. A variety's\
      \ total pending equals `max(0, −stock)` with a withdrawal involved.\n- `stock_level`:\n\
      \  - withdrawn grams pace days left; an adjustment does not;\n  - a variety\
      \ with only a withdrawal is sold out, not \"no entries\";\n  - sold-out-since\
      \ follows a withdrawal's date.\n- `draw_source_of` maps both reasons.\n\nAcceptance:\
      \ `mypy --strict` and `ruff` clean; existing stock and results tests pass unchanged."
    seams:
    - app.stock.domain.lot_order.lots_and_draws
    - app.stock.domain.allocation.allocate
    - app.stock.domain.stock_level.stock_level
    - app.stock.domain.draw_source.draw_source_of
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-83.stock-draw-contract
    base: fd138d3
    reviewed_head: 9c58e358e3a4ceec1bb13f3388e414a5c6e1b058
  stock-withdrawal-aggregate:
    id: stock-withdrawal-aggregate
    title: 'Stock domain: the Withdrawal aggregate and its refusals'
    brief: "New `app/stock/domain/withdrawal.py`; new error classes in `app/stock/domain/errors.py`\
      \ (or `withdrawal.py`, as long as stock's error module stays one place to look).\n\
      \n- `WithdrawalLine` value object: `variety_id: int`, `grams: Grams`. Grams\
      \ must be above zero (`WithdrawalGramsNotPositive`). More than two decimals\
      \ is `WithdrawalGramsTooPrecise`: how a raw grams input becomes `Grams` must\
      \ refuse it with this class. Check how `Grams` parsing works today and raise\
      \ the withdrawal's own class.\n- `Withdrawal` aggregate:\n  - `id: int | None`,\
      \ `reason: WithdrawalReason` (import it from `app/stock/domain/draw_source.py`,\
      \ which `stock-draw-contract` creates; if that subtask has not merged yet, create\
      \ the enum there with exactly the values `promotion` and `own_use`);\n  - `withdrawn_on:\
      \ date`, `note: str | None`, `lines: tuple[WithdrawalLine, ...]`, `recorded_at:\
      \ datetime`.\n  - Its constructor (or a `record(...)` factory taking `today`)\
      \ enforces:\n    - a reason is required: a missing or unknown raw value is `WithdrawalReasonRequired`;\n\
      \    - a date after `today` is `WithdrawalDateInFuture`;\n    - no lines is\
      \ `WithdrawalNoLines`;\n    - the note is trimmed, blank means `None`, and over\
      \ 200 characters is `WithdrawalNoteTooLong` (reuse `NOTE_MAX_LENGTH` from `entry.py`\
      \ if it is 200).\n  - The same variety may appear in several lines, with no\
      \ merging.\n  - An edit builds a new `Withdrawal` with the same validation,\
      \ keeping `id` and `recorded_at`.\n  - `total_grams` sums the lines (used by\
      \ the front's delete confirmation through the API).\n- Errors: `WithdrawalNotFound`\
      \ and `WithdrawalVarietyNotFound` are `LookupError`s (kind gone). Every class\
      \ carries `code: ClassVar[str]` with these codes:\n  - `stock.withdrawal_reason_required`\n\
      \  - `stock.withdrawal_date_in_future`\n  - `stock.withdrawal_no_lines`\n  -\
      \ `stock.withdrawal_grams_not_positive`\n  - `stock.withdrawal_grams_too_precise`\n\
      \  - `stock.withdrawal_note_too_long`\n  - `stock.withdrawal_not_found`\n  -\
      \ `stock.withdrawal_variety_not_found`\n\n  Every message is fixed: no note,\
      \ grams or ids.\n- Do NOT add the codes to `web/src/api/contract.json` here.\
      \ `stock-withdrawal-api` adds them in the same diff that registers the classes\
      \ on the router, since `tests/test_refusal_codes.py` checks registered classes\
      \ against the JSON.\n\nTests: domain unit tests for every refusal and for the\
      \ accepted edge cases (today's date, exactly 200 characters, repeated variety,\
      \ 0.01 g). Assert that no message contains the input."
    seams:
    - app.stock.domain.withdrawal.Withdrawal
    depends_on: []
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-83.stock-withdrawal-aggregate
    base: fd138d3
    reviewed_head: 179776c9910643c3efc849935588ebdaacac009b
  withdrawal-tables:
    id: withdrawal-tables
    title: Tables, migration 0012 and business-removal labels for withdrawals
    brief: "Files: `app/stock/adapters/tables.py`; new `app/migrations/versions/0012_stock_withdrawals.py`\
      \ (revises 0011); `app/business_removal/domain/labels.py`; `app/tables.py` /\
      \ `TABLE_MODULES` only if a new module is added (prefer keeping the classes\
      \ in stock's `tables.py`).\n\n- `StockWithdrawalRow` (`stock_withdrawal`):\n\
      \  - `id` pk; `business_id` FK `business.id` not null;\n  - `reason` String\
      \ (length enough for `own_use`);\n  - `withdrawn_on` date; `note` String(200)\
      \ nullable; `recorded_at` `UtcDateTime`;\n  - `CheckConstraint(\"reason IN ('promotion',\
      \ 'own_use')\", \"reason\")`: the short name only, no `name=`, so the convention\
      \ gives `ck_stock_withdrawal_reason`.\n- `StockWithdrawalLineRow` (`stock_withdrawal_line`):\n\
      \  - `id` pk; `business_id` FK not null; `stock_withdrawal_id` FK `stock_withdrawal.id`\
      \ not null; `variety_id` FK `variety.id` not null;\n  - `position` int; `grams_hundredths`\
      \ int;\n  - `UniqueConstraint(\"stock_withdrawal_id\", \"position\")` and `Index(None,\
      \ \"business_id\", \"variety_id\")`.\n  - Plain foreign keys, no cascade, no\
      \ ORM relationship.\n- The migration creates both tables with `op.create_table`\
      \ and spells every convention name with `op.f(...)`. Downgrade drops them.\n\
      - Business removal: `LABEL_COLUMN[\"stock_withdrawal\"] = \"withdrawn_on\"`,\
      \ `LABEL_VIA[\"stock_withdrawal_line\"] = \"variety_id\"`.\n- §11 applies to\
      \ every name. The `test_naming.py` allowlist is untouched.\n\nTests:\n- `tests/migrations/test_naming.py`,\
      \ `test_business_ownership.py` and the schema test (migrations equal tables)\
      \ pass with the new tables.\n- A new schema test asserts that the reflected\
      \ check constraint's value list equals `{r.value for r in WithdrawalReason}`.\n\
      - Business removal's own migrated-schema twin tests pass: no unlabelled table,\
      \ no secret or OTHER-typed column."
    seams:
    - the migrated schema reflected by upgrade_to_head (tests/migrations)
    - app.business_removal.domain.labels.unlabelled_tables
    depends_on:
    - stock-withdrawal-aggregate
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-83.withdrawal-tables
    base: 9c58e35
    conflicted_paths: []
    reviewed_head: ac1741f6124450dba24654699b9e213de13bc025
  results-buckets:
    id: results-buckets
    title: 'Results: draw rows by source, withdrawal buckets, profit and first record'
    brief: "Files: `app/stock/adapters/draw_rows.py`; `app/results/domain/period_figures.py`\
      \ (the `Buckets`/`scan`/`totals_between` part only); `app/results/domain/figures.py`\
      \ (only `PeriodTotals` and `AmountWithPending`, if a helper is needed).\n\n\
      - `build_draw_rows`: replace the nested ternary with `match draw.source`, ending\
      \ in `assert_never`:\n  - `SALE_LINE` → `gifted` when the stored pricing rule\
      \ is gift, else `sold`, with variety, sale and line total as today;\n  - `ADJUSTMENT`\
      \ → `shortfall`;\n  - `PROMOTION` → `promotion`;\n  - `OWN_USE` → `own_use`.\n\
      \n  The last three carry `variety_id`, `variety_name`, `sale_id` and `line_total`\
      \ as `None`. The kind is never told by a missing field.\n- `period_figures.py`:\n\
      \  - Rename `_ShortfallDayTotals` to `_CostDayTotals(costed, pending_grams)`.\n\
      \  - `Buckets` gains `withdrawal_totals: dict[tuple[date, Literal[\"promotion\"\
      ,\"own_use\"]], _CostDayTotals]`.\n  - One private `_cost_line(...) -> AmountWithPending\
      \ | None` answers `shortfall`, `promotion` and `own_use`, each `None` exactly\
      \ when no draw of that kind falls in the window.\n  - `scan` loops over draws\
      \ with `match row.kind` ending in `assert_never`:\n    - `shortfall` as today;\n\
      \    - `promotion` and `own_use` write only `withdrawal_totals` and fold `row.day`\
      \ into `first_record`;\n    - `sold` and `gifted` as today.\n\n    Withdrawal\
      \ rows never touch `day_totals`, `variety_day_totals`, `sales_by_day` or the\
      \ weekday buckets.\n  - `Buckets.profit` stays the one formula. It subtracts\
      \ the costed promotion and own-use amounts and adds both reasons' pending grams\
      \ to profit's pending.\n  - `PeriodTotals` gains `promotion` and `own_use` (each\
      \ `AmountWithPending | None`), filled by `totals_between`.\n  - Do NOT change\
      \ the month, week, months and since-first-record builders; `results-windows`\
      \ does those.\n\nTests (domain, over `DrawRow` fixtures and `build_draw_rows`\
      \ over hand-built lots and draws):\n- each source maps to its kind;\n- a promotion\
      \ and an own-use row each lower profit by their costed amount on their own date;\n\
      - `shortfall` and cost of goods are unchanged by a withdrawal, as are sales,\
      \ grams sold or gifted, and weekday sales;\n- a pending-only withdrawal gives\
      \ profit a pending count and leaves cost of goods with no pending;\n- each reason's\
      \ pending counts only its own grams, and profit adds all (the PRD's 4 g + 2\
      \ g + 1 g = 7 g example);\n- a withdrawal dated before every other record moves\
      \ `first_record`;\n- a window with no withdrawal of a reason answers `None`\
      \ for it."
    seams:
    - app.stock.adapters.draw_rows.build_draw_rows
    - app.results.domain.period_figures.scan
    - app.results.domain.period_figures.totals_between
    depends_on:
    - stock-draw-contract
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-83.results-buckets
    base: 9c58e35
    reviewed_head: 2dd0d0b657620eef161b05bcf099930aeaaac9f8
    reason: 'Fix `_add_sale_line` in `period_figures.py:263`/`:273`: both branch on
      `DrawKind` with `if`/`else`, which breaks invariant 1. Everything else in the
      brief was checked and holds, and the seam tests pass.'
    return_category: plan-invariant
  stock-history:
    id: stock-history
    title: Withdrawal repository, history() and the five readers
    brief: "Files:\n- new `app/stock/adapters/sql_stock_withdrawals.py`;\n- `app/stock/application/ports.py`\
      \ (new `Withdrawals` and `WithdrawalDraws` protocols);\n- new `app/stock/application/history.py`;\n\
      - `read_stock.py`, `line_costs.py`, `draw_costs.py`, `stock_figures.py`.\n\n\
      - `SqlStockWithdrawals(session, business_id)` implements both ports. Every statement\
      \ is filtered on `business_id`, the `ON` clauses of any join included.\n  -\
      \ `by_id(id) -> Withdrawal | None`: another business's id answers `None`.\n\
      \  - `add(withdrawal) -> Withdrawal`: header, then lines with positions `0..n-1`\
      \ in the given order.\n  - `replace(withdrawal) -> Withdrawal`:\n    - update\
      \ the header; `id` and `recorded_at` never change;\n    - run a Core `sa_delete(StockWithdrawalLineRow).where(business_id,\
      \ stock_withdrawal_id)` through `session.execute` before adding any line;\n\
      \    - then insert the new lines and flush.\n  - `remove(id)`: lines then header,\
      \ both Core deletes.\n  - `for_varieties(variety_ids | None) -> list[WithdrawalLineDraw]`,\
      \ plus a read for the movements list that `stock-withdrawal-api` needs: the\
      \ withdrawals touching one variety, with that variety's summed grams per withdrawal.\n\
      \  - `SqlStockWithdrawals._reason_of(row)` is the only place `WithdrawalReason(row.reason)`\
      \ is built. It has no fallback.\n  - Repositories flush and never commit.\n\
      - `history(entries, draws, withdrawal_draws, variety_ids: Collection[int] |\
      \ None)` reads the three sources with the same `variety_ids` and returns `lots_and_draws(...)`.\
      \ It is the only caller of `lots_and_draws` in the codebase. The five readers\
      \ call `history` and no longer call `lots_and_draws` or a source's `for_varieties`\
      \ themselves.\n\nTests, at the repository and `history` seams over a fresh in-memory\
      \ SQLite with the real adapters:\n- add, then read back with lines in order;\n\
      - a replace with the same number of lines, and one that reverses their order,\
      \ never trips the unique constraint;\n- a foreign business's withdrawal is invisible\
      \ to `by_id` and `for_varieties`;\n- `history` narrowed to one variety and un-narrowed\
      \ give that variety's draws in the same order and cost.\n\nHTTP behaviour tests\
      \ come in `stock-withdrawal-api`."
    seams:
    - app.stock.adapters.sql_stock_withdrawals.SqlStockWithdrawals
    - app.stock.application.history.history
    depends_on:
    - stock-draw-contract
    - withdrawal-tables
    - results-buckets
    status: approved
    branch: agentic/GH-83.stock-history
    base: 21c9bb7
    reviewed_head: 5bd835d70e18383b2d4d9e45d6a63c0fc930074c
  stock-withdrawal-api:
    id: stock-withdrawal-api
    title: Withdrawal use cases, routes, refusal codes and HTTP behaviour
    brief: "Files:\n- new `app/stock/application/manage_withdrawals.py`;\n- `app/stock/adapters/router.py`\
      \ and `schemas.py`;\n- `web/src/api/contract.json` (refusal codes only);\n-\
      \ new fixtures `web/src/api/fixtures/withdrawal.json` and `movements.json`;\n\
      - `tests/contract_nullable.py`, plus `FIXTURE_ROUTES` in `tests/test_contract_shapes.py`;\n\
      - `tests/test_business_isolation.py` (seeder and `BODIES`).\n\n- Use cases:\
      \ `record_withdrawal`, `edit_withdrawal`, `delete_withdrawal`, `read_withdrawal`,\
      \ `read_movements`.\n  - Each one that writes commits once, at its end, through\
      \ `Transaction`.\n  - Each line's variety is checked through `VarietyCatalog.exists`;\
      \ an unknown one raises `WithdrawalVarietyNotFound`.\n  - `today` and `now`\
      \ come from the router's `TodayDep` and `Clock`, as the stock entry routes do.\
      \ `recorded_at` is set only on record.\n  - A withdrawal is never refused for\
      \ lack of stock or for grams already drawn.\n- Routes on `stock_router`, which\
      \ is already included with `upkeep_gated`:\n  - `POST /stock/withdrawals` →\
      \ 201 with the withdrawal;\n  - `GET /stock/withdrawals/{withdrawal_id}`;\n\
      \  - `PUT /stock/withdrawals/{withdrawal_id}`, with the whole withdrawal in\
      \ the body;\n  - `DELETE /stock/withdrawals/{withdrawal_id}` → 204;\n  - `GET\
      \ /stock/varieties/{variety_id}/movements`.\n- Schemas stay bare (ADR-0005).\n\
      \  - Body: `reason: str | None`, `date`, `note: str | None`, `lines: [{variety_id,\
      \ grams}]`, with grams in the same input shape stock entries use.\n  - Answer:\
      \ `{id, reason, date, note, total_grams, lines: [{variety_id, variety_name,\
      \ grams}]}`.\n  - Movements: a list, newest first, ordered on the server by\
      \ (day desc, recorded_at desc, kind, id desc). Each item is `{kind: \"entry\"\
      |\"withdrawal\", id, date, ...}`. Entries carry the fields their history row\
      \ needs today: origin, grams, cost. Withdrawals carry `reason` and that variety's\
      \ summed grams in it. One row per withdrawal, even with repeated lines.\n- `TranslateErrors`\
      \ registers the eight withdrawal classes (404 for the two gone ones). Add the\
      \ eight codes to `contract.json` in this same diff.\n\nHTTP tests (`TestClient`,\
      \ `client`/`other_client`, movable clock):\n- the validation matrix, one coded\
      \ refusal per case, and no body containing the note, grams or amounts;\n- the\
      \ clock at 2026-10-06T01:00Z refuses a withdrawal dated 06/10 and accepts 05/10;\n\
      - record, edit and delete move `GET /stock` exactly (in minus out, to the hundredth);\
      \ a withdrawal past stock is recorded and shows sold out;\n- days left, \"no\
      \ entries\" and sold-out-since behave as stock.md says;\n- an edit with the\
      \ same line count, and one with reversed lines, answers 200;\n- `GET /sales/{id}`:\
      \ a line's cost moves when an earlier-dated withdrawal on its variety is recorded,\
      \ edited and deleted, and equals the same line's cost seen through `GET /analysis`'s\
      \ month (the five readers agree);\n- after a withdrawal: sales list, sales totals\
      \ and count, customer balances and account, and Caja are unchanged;\n- movements\
      \ mix entries and withdrawals in order, with a withdrawal touching two varieties\
      \ shown on each;\n- pinned-catalog trigger matrix, with the fake Telegram adapter:\n\
      \  - an edit moving the only line from A to B edits the pin once, and the text\
      \ lacks A;\n  - a delete that brings a variety back above zero edits it;\n \
      \ - a note-only edit sends nothing;\n  - every `/stock/withdrawals` write route\
      \ carries `schedule_upkeep`;\n- isolation sweep: seed a withdrawal on the `MARKER`\
      \ variety; every new route answers 404 or empty from the other business, including\
      \ a body naming the other business's variety;\n- gated routes;\n- business removal's\
      \ \"nothing survives\" and export = delete, seeded with a two-line withdrawal;\n\
      - answers match the new fixtures through `assert_matches_fixture`."
    seams:
    - POST /stock/withdrawals
    - GET /stock/withdrawals/{withdrawal_id}
    - PUT /stock/withdrawals/{withdrawal_id}
    - DELETE /stock/withdrawals/{withdrawal_id}
    - GET /stock/varieties/{variety_id}/movements
    - GET /stock
    - GET /sales/{sale_id}
    depends_on:
    - stock-history
    - stock-withdrawal-aggregate
    status: approved

    id: results-windows
    title: 'Results: Promoción and Consumo propio in month, weeks, months and since
      the first record'
    brief: "Files: `app/results/domain/period_figures.py` (the window builders: `_month_figures`,\
      \ `_weeks`, `_months`, the since-first-record figures, `period_figures`); `app/results/domain/figures.py`.\n\
      \n- `MonthFigures` (the chosen month's header), `WeekFigures`, the month-row\
      \ figures and the since-first-record totals each gain `promotion` and `own_use`\
      \ (`AmountWithPending | None`). They read the same `Buckets` helpers `results-buckets`\
      \ added, never a second sum.\n- `first_record` already includes a withdrawal's\
      \ day, so these follow from it with no new code:\n  - history starts at a withdrawal\
      \ dated before everything else;\n  - a business whose only record is a withdrawal\
      \ has its week and month, with $0 sales and negative profit;\n  - \"nothing\
      \ to analyse\" shows only with no record at all.\n- Variety rows, share, grams\
      \ sold or gifted, average margin, best week and best weekday keep reading only\
      \ sale-line buckets.\n\nTests (domain, over `DrawRow` and record fixtures through\
      \ `period_figures`):\n- each window shows each reason's line only when present,\
      \ with its own pending grams;\n- profit in each window equals `totals_between`\
      \ for the same range;\n- a withdrawal moves no variety row, share, average margin,\
      \ best week or best weekday;\n- a withdrawal as the only record starts the history;\n\
      - the PRD's 7 g / 2 g / 1 g week reads exactly so."

    depends_on:

  results-answers:
    id: results-answers
    title: Panel and analysis answers carry the two lines; agreement through HTTP
    brief: "Files:\n- `app/results/adapters/schemas.py` and `assemble.py`, plus `app/results/application/read_panel.py`\
      \ and `analyse.py` only if a field needs passing through;\n- fixtures `web/src/api/fixtures/panel.json`,\
      \ `analysis.json` and `analysis-empty.json`;\n- `tests/contract_nullable.py`.\n\
      \n- Panel: generalise `ShortfallOut` into `CostLineOut {amount, pending: bool}`,\
      \ with no grams on the wire. Each period gains `promotion` and `own_use` as\
      \ `CostLineOut | null`; `shortfall` keeps its key and shape. `pending` comes\
      \ from each line's own pending grams, and `profit_pending` from profit's.\n\
      - Analysis: the month header, each week row, each month row and the since-first-record\
      \ totals gain `promotion` and `own_use` as `AmountWithPendingOut | null`, with\
      \ pending grams as on shortfall.\n- Hoy keeps answering the field from the same\
      \ `PeriodTotals`. That the screen hides it on Hoy is the front's rule from results.md,\
      \ not the API's.\n\nHTTP tests (`TestClient`, clock on a Friday so Hoy, week\
      \ and month differ):\n- Seed a history holding:\n  - a costed withdrawal of\
      \ each reason;\n  - a withdrawal with pending grams;\n  - a negative adjustment\
      \ with costed grams and one with pending grams;\n  - a sale line with pending\
      \ grams.\n\n  Over it, `GET /panel`'s week and month equal `GET /analysis`'s\
      \ rows for Ganancia, cost of goods, shortfall, Promoción, Consumo propio, expenses\
      \ and Caja, with 0 differences.\n- A withdrawal dated before every other record\
      \ starts Análisis's history and agrees with the panel's week and month.\n- A\
      \ business whose only record is a withdrawal answers that history.\n- After\
      \ a withdrawal, sales, count, average, grams sold, Regalaste, cost of goods,\
      \ shortfall and Caja are unchanged on both routes.\n- The statement-count tests\
      \ for both routes still hold: the count stays the same small or seeded, with\
      \ its new constant.\n- Isolation: the other business reads both routes as empty\
      \ or zero.\n- Answers match the updated fixtures."
    seams:
    - GET /panel
    - GET /analysis
    depends_on:
    - results-windows
    - stock-withdrawal-api
    status: approved

  front-withdrawal-form:
    id: front-withdrawal-form
    title: 'Front: Sacar del stock form, withdrawal detail, Más and Variedades entries'
    brief: "Waits for #59 on master (the navigation shell).\n\nFiles:\n- `web/src/api/stock.ts`:\
      \ the withdrawal, line and movement wire types, hand-written to match `withdrawal.json`\
      \ and `movements.json`; the movement type is shared with `front-results-and-history`;\n\
      - new screens for the form and the detail;\n- `App.tsx` routes;\n- Más and Variedades\
      \ (phone and computer);\n- `contract.json` (`frontSegments` gains `withdrawals`)\
      \ and `web/src/api/refusals.ts` (Spanish copy for the eight codes);\n- `scripts/design/screens.json`\
      \ entries.\n\n- **`/withdrawals/new`**, the form \"Sacar del stock\". It is\
      \ a form under the navigation rule: \"Cerrar\", no bar, no \"+ Nueva venta\"\
      , and the leave guard (`useLeaveGuard`) when something is typed.\n  - Reason:\
      \ Promoción · Consumo propio, required, no default.\n  - Date: today, never\
      \ later.\n  - One or more lines of variety and grams, repeated varieties allowed,\
      \ with \"Agregar otra variedad\".\n  - Note.\n  - `?variety=<id>` pre-chooses\
      \ the first line's variety.\n  - #80 phone standard: 44 px targets, inputs at\
      \ least 16 px, grams `inputmode=\"decimal\"`, `enterkeyhint` through a line.\n\
      \  - Any sheet inside it (picking a variety) uses `useSheet` (ADR-0027).\n \
      \ - Save shows the toast \"Retiro guardado\" and goes back by history.\n  -\
      \ Refusals show inline and keep what was typed.\n- **`/withdrawals/:id`**, the\
      \ detail. It shows the bar or sidebar and a \"‹\" back to the variety's page\
      \ it came from, or Variedades with no history, and no \"+ Nueva venta\".\n \
      \ - It shows the date, the reason by word, every line and the note.\n  - \"\
      Editar\" opens the same form filled in. Saving an edit is confirmed first.\n\
      \  - \"Borrar\" asks \"¿Borrás la promoción del dd/mm? Vuelven N g al stock.\"\
      \ or \"¿Borrás el consumo propio del dd/mm? …\", with \"No, dejalo / Sí, borralo\"\
      . Then the toast \"Retiro eliminado\".\n- Gone (404):\n  - opening the detail\
      \ shows \"Eso ya no está.\" with \"Volver a Variedades\";\n  - saving, deleting\
      \ or confirming on a gone withdrawal shows \"Eso ya no está. La lista ya está\
      \ al día.\".\n- Más: \"Sacar del stock\" second, right after \"+ Cargar gasto\"\
      . Variedades (phone and computer): a secondary \"Sacar del stock\" next to \"\
      + Entrada de stock\". Both open the form with no variety chosen.\n- Formats\
      \ come from the shared format module. Nothing goes to the console or storage.\n\
      \nTests (Testing Library, `fetch` faked with the fixtures):\n- form chrome and\
      \ the discard confirmation, plus the untouched close;\n- the reason is required;\n\
      - the toasts;\n- edit and delete confirmations with their fixed text;\n- both\
      \ gone forms;\n- the Más order;\n- the Variedades button;\n- a refusal keeps\
      \ the fields.\n\nLook at the design comparisons before delivering."
    seams:
    - the withdrawal form screen (route /withdrawals/new) through Testing Library
    - the withdrawal detail screen (route /withdrawals/:id) through Testing Library
    - the Más and Variedades screens through Testing Library
    depends_on:
    - stock-withdrawal-api
    status: approved
    screens:
    - SacarDelStock
    - SacarDelStockCompu
    - RetiroDetalle
    - Mas
    - Variedades
    - VariedadesCompu
    branch: agentic/GH-83.front-withdrawal-form

  front-results-and-history:
    id: front-results-and-history
    title: 'Front: Promoción and Consumo propio on Inicio and Análisis; movements
      on a variety''s page'
    brief: "Waits for #59 on master.\n\nFiles:\n- `web/src/api/panel.ts`: generalise\
      \ `PanelShortfall` into the `CostLineOut` twin and add `promotion` and `own_use`\
      \ per period;\n- `web/src/api/analysis.ts`: the new fields in the month header,\
      \ week and month rows, and since-first-record totals;\n- the movement type from\
      \ `web/src/api/stock.ts`, which `front-withdrawal-form` writes; if it has not\
      \ merged, define it there exactly per `movements.json`;\n- the Inicio, Análisis\
      \ and VariedadDetalle screens.\n\n- **Inicio**, Semana and Mes only:\n  - \"\
      Promoción $ X\" and \"Consumo propio $ X\", each only when the period has one,\
      \ right after \"Faltante de stock\" and before \"Gastos\";\n  - the fixed \"\
      incluye costo pendiente\" note under each line when its `pending` is true;\n\
      \  - Ganancia's note from `profit_pending`.\n\n  Hoy shows neither.\n- **Análisis**:\
      \ the two lines apart from \"Faltante de stock\", each with its own \"y N g\
      \ sin costo\", in the month header, each week and month row, and the since-first-record\
      \ totals.\n- **A variety's page**:\n  - a \"Sacar del stock\" action next to\
      \ \"+ Entrada\" and \"Nuevo precio\", opening `/withdrawals/new?variety=<id>`;\n\
      \  - \"Stock y entradas\" and its \"Ver todas\" read `GET /stock/varieties/{id}/movements`;\n\
      \  - a withdrawal row shows the date, \"Promoción\" or \"Consumo propio\" and\
      \ \"−N g\", told by word and sign, never by colour, and opens `/withdrawals/:id`.\n\
      \n  \"Todas las entradas\" stays entries only.\n- Every figure is the API's.\
      \ The browser computes nothing.\n\nTests (Testing Library, `fetch` faked with\
      \ the fixtures):\n- Inicio's line order, and Hoy without the lines;\n- notes\
      \ driven by each flag;\n- Análisis shows each reason's line and pending text\
      \ only when present;\n- the variety page's action target;\n- movement rows told\
      \ apart by word and sign;\n- \"Todas las entradas\" holds no withdrawal.\n\n\
      Look at the design comparisons for these screens before delivering."
    seams:
    - the Inicio screen through Testing Library
    - the Análisis screen through Testing Library
    - the variety page (VariedadDetalle) through Testing Library

    screens:

    - InicioOscuro

invariants:
- Every branch over `DrawSource` or results' `DrawKind` is an exhaustive `match` ending
  in `assert_never`, with no default branch.
- A draw's kind is decided only from `Draw.source`, never from a missing `sale_id`,
  pricing rule or line total.
- '`lots_and_draws` is called only from `app/stock/application/history.py::history`,
  and its withdrawal argument has no default.'
- '`draw_source_of` is called only from `lots_and_draws`; `WithdrawalReason(row.reason)`
  is built only in `SqlStockWithdrawals._reason_of`.'
- '`STOCK_ENTRY_RANK` stays 0 and `SALE_LINE_RANK` stays 1; `WITHDRAWAL_LINE_RANK`
  is 2.'
- No profit, cost, pending or first-record figure is summed or folded outside `app/results/domain/period_figures.py`;
  profit has one formula, in `Buckets.profit`.
- A `promotion` or `own_use` draw row never writes the sale-line, variety, weekday
  or sales buckets.
- Every new table carries a non-null `business_id`, a declared business-removal label,
  and §11 names produced by `NAMING_CONVENTION` (no `name=`), spelled with `op.f(...)`
  in the migration.
- Every repository statement over the withdrawal tables filters on the repository's
  `business_id`, join `ON` clauses included; another business's withdrawal or variety
  answers 404.
- Replacing a withdrawal's lines runs a Core delete through `session.execute` before
  any line is added; `id` and `recorded_at` never change on edit.
- Every withdrawal write route lives on `stock_router`, so `schedule_upkeep` runs
  after commit; no use case reports varieties to upkeep.
- The future-date check and `recorded_at` come from the one injected clock, in Argentina
  time.
- Every refusal class carries a `code` listed in `contract.json` in the same diff
  that registers it, and its message names no note, grams, amount or id.
- No module under `app/sales`, `app/payments` or `app/customers` reads a withdrawal
  table or type.
- Front wire types live only in `web/src/api/` modules and match a fixture checked
  by `tests/test_contract_shapes.py`; no screen declares its own.
integration_brief: 'After both front subtasks merge:

  - Add the end-to-end front run (phone and computer viewports) that the PRD''s "No
  curl" and tap-budget metrics ask for. It records a withdrawal from Más and from
  a variety''s page, edits and deletes one, and reads Promoción and Consumo propio
  on Inicio and Análisis.

  - Confirm the e2e storage helper still finds only `tema`.

  - Check that the variety page''s "Sacar del stock" action and its movement rows
  (from `front-results-and-history`) open the routes built by `front-withdrawal-form`
  (`/withdrawals/new?variety=` and `/withdrawals/:id`).

  - Check that `scripts/design/screens.json` holds `route = path` for the new screens.

  - Run `scripts/design/compare.sh` before and after, for the form, the detail, Más,
  Variedades, a variety''s page, Inicio and Análisis.'

- subtask: stock-draw-contract
  version: 1
  location: app/stock/domain/lot_order.py:151
  claim: The `lots_and_draws` docstring says the function is "called only from `history()`"
    and the module docstring says the same, but `history()` does not exist yet. On
    this branch its five callers pass `withdrawal_draws=[]` (as the brief says to),
    so invariant 3 only holds once `stock-history` lands.

  scope: plan

  status: open
- subtask: stock-draw-contract
  version: 1
  location: app/stock/adapters/line_costs.py:39
  claim: Older code that this diff did not change still picks a sale's draws by `draw.order.rank
    == SALE_LINE_RANK` rather than `draw.source`. Withdrawal draws sort at rank 2,
    so the result is correct, but `stock-history` could switch it to `DrawSource.SALE_LINE`
    to match invariant 2.
  severity: observation
  scope: plan
  category: standard-other
  status: dismissed
- subtask: stock-draw-contract
  version: 1
  location: app/stock/domain/lot_order.py:112
  claim: '`WithdrawalLineDraw` has no `__post_init__` check for positive grams. It
    follows the `SaleLineDraw` precedent, and `Draw.__post_init__` already rejects
    grams that are not positive when the draw is built.'
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: standard-other
  status: dismissed
- subtask: stock-withdrawal-aggregate
  version: 1
  location: app/stock/domain/withdrawal.py:47
  claim: Precision is checked before positivity, so a negative input with more than
    two decimals (e.g. "-0.001") gets `stock.withdrawal_grams_too_precise` rather
    than `grams_not_positive`; the brief does not say which should win.
  severity: observation
  scope: subtask
  category: spec-behaviour
  status: dismissed
- subtask: stock-withdrawal-aggregate
