# Stock withdrawals: grams taken out for promotion or own use, with their own lines in Ganancia

## Original request

Grams also leave stock without anyone buying them: the seller takes a few grams to a party so several people can try them (to make the business known), or consumes them personally. Today the only way to record that is a sale without a customer with gift lines, which counts a sale nobody made, or a negative adjustment, which reads as missing stock. Decided in a grill with Zeta (2026-10-05).

### Glossary (CONTEXT.md)

- **Stock withdrawal** (new): grams the business takes out of its own stock with no sale and no customer, for one reason: promotion or own use. A date, one or more varieties with their grams, and an optional note. Like a draw, it takes from the oldest lots first; a part beyond every lot is pending cost. It may be edited or deleted at any time, and the lots after it are worked out again (as ADR-0009 does for stock entries). _In the app_: Sacar del stock. _Avoid_: outflow, consumption, write-off.
- **Promotion** (new): a stock withdrawal to make the business known, e.g. grams shared at a party for several people to try. _In the app_: Promoción. _Avoid_: gift (a gift is a sale line, to someone, inside a sale), sample, freebie.
- **Own use** (new): a stock withdrawal the seller consumes personally. _In the app_: Consumo propio. _Avoid_: personal, shrinkage.
- **Gift**: add "Grams shared to promote the business are a promotion, never a gift." and drop "promo" from its _Avoid_ list (promotion is now its own term).
- **Draw**: add "or a stock withdrawal" to what counts as grams going out.
- **Stock**: everything that came in minus everything sold, gifted **or withdrawn**.
- **Profit**: sales − cost of goods sold − stock shortfall − **promotion cost − own-use cost** − expenses. Promotion cost and own-use cost are what the withdrawn grams cost from their lots as they stand today, each on its own line, never folded into cost of goods sold; pending grams join the "y N g sin costo" count. Caja is unaffected (no money moves).
- **Days of stock** ("Al ritmo de los últimos 30 días"): withdrawn grams count as grams going out.
- **Stock status** "no entries": a withdrawal counts as movement.

### Expected

- Record, edit and delete a stock withdrawal (reason Promoción or Consumo propio; date; one or more variety + grams lines; optional note) through the API, scoped to the business like everything else.
- Lots, stock, pending cost and the days-of-stock rate take withdrawals into account; editing or deleting one re-works the lots after it.
- Profit (Inicio and Análisis) shows **Promoción** and **Consumo propio** as their own lines, at lot cost, pending marked as today.
- A "Sacar del stock" form, opened from Más and from a variety's page, following the form rule of the navigation redesign (#58/#59) and the mobile standard (#80); withdrawals show in the variety's history.
- Withdrawals never count as sales: not in sales count, sales totals, average sale or any customer's history.

### Out of scope

Who attended or how many tried; tying a promotion to later sales; money spent on a party (that is an expense).

Waits for #59 (the screen follows the new navigation); the backend and glossary could land first.

## PjM

New behaviour with product value: users can record stock withdrawals and see Promoción and Consumo propio as separate lines in Ganancia. The glossary (Stock withdrawal, Promotion, Own use, Profit, Stock, Draw) has to change, so this is a `feature`.

## Interview: Product Analyst→PM

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1: the job to be done

The `grilling` and `domain-modeling` skills are not installed here, so I'm following the question format from my brief. The glossary and ADRs are in the context, and I read them before asking. This round is only about the job. Behaviour comes in the next rounds.

What the product has today for this situation:
- A **gift** is a sale line to someone, inside a **sale** (CONTEXT.md: Gift, Sale). A sale with no customer must be paid in full, so a gift-only sale is a sale nobody made. It enters the sales count, the average sale and Ventas.
- A **negative adjustment** is "the difference between a physical count and the stock the app shows". Its cost is the **stock shortfall**, and it lowers profit (Adjustment, Stock shortfall). So it reads as missing flower, which is the opposite of what happened.
- Ganancia today is sales − cost of goods sold − stock shortfall − expenses. Neither promotion nor own use has a line of its own.

❓ **Q1** - **Situation**: In which moment does the seller reach for "Sacar del stock"? I assume two cases: (a) just after handing grams out for people to try, at a party or a meeting, or when the seller takes some for personal use, with the grams already gone; and (b) later, when catching up on the day's records. Is it one of these, or both? Is it always after the fact, never before?
➡️ Recommendation: Both, always after the fact. It is recorded from the phone in a few taps, between orders. That fits BRIEF's stock job ("so stock per variety stays right") and the phone-first rule.

❓ **Q2** - **Progress wanted**: What does the seller want to get out of recording it? I see three possibilities: (1) stock per variety stays right, with no false "sold" or "missing" entries; (2) Ganancia shows what promotion and own use cost, as separate lines; (3) sales figures stay clean. Which matter, and which is the main one?
➡️ Recommendation: All three, with (2) as the headline. The seller wants to see "how much did promoting and consuming cost me" without it hiding inside cost of goods sold or inflating sales. The job story I propose:

> **When** I take some grams out of my stock without selling them (to share at a party so people get to know the business, or for my own use), **I want** to record the grams by variety with the reason, in a few taps, **so I can** keep my stock right and see in Ganancia what promotion and my own consumption cost me, without registering a sale nobody made or an adjustment that looks like lost flower.

❓ **Q3** - **How they judge it went well**: I propose these success criteria:
1. A withdrawal is recorded from the phone in under a minute.
2. After recording, the variety's stock and days left reflect it, and Ganancia (Inicio and Análisis) shows "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" as separate lines at lot cost. Pending cost is marked as today.
3. No sales figure changes: sales count, sales total, average sale and every customer's history stay exactly as before.

Which of these would you drop or change? Is there another test the seller would apply, for example "I can tell this month's promotion cost at a glance"?
➡️ Recommendation: Keep the three as written. Add nothing about who attended or about later sales (already out of scope).

❓ **Q4** - **What they do today**: As I understand it, Zeta records these grams today as a sale without a customer with gift lines, or as a negative adjustment, and lives with the distortion. Is that right? Or do some grams not get recorded at all, so the stock figure drifts until the next count?
➡️ Recommendation: I'll write that today the seller either distorts the books (a phantom sale, or "missing stock") or skips recording and corrects stock at the next count. That is the problem statement for the PRD. Tell me if the second case doesn't happen.

### Round 1: answers

### Round 1: the job to be done

**Q1**: Accepted, with one clarification. The withdrawal records grams that have already left stock. The seller records it with the date the grams left: either right away from the phone, or later when catching up, by changing the date to an earlier day (never a future date, as for every other record). If the seller takes grams to a party the day before, the grams have left stock that day, so that is the withdrawal's date. There is no "planned" or draft withdrawal.

**Q2**: Accepted. All three outcomes matter. The headline is honest books: stock right per variety, and Ganancia showing on their own lines what promotion and own use cost. Clean sales figures is the condition that makes both of those true. This is the job story, in my words, and it is settled:

> **When** I take grams out of my own stock without selling them (to share at a party so people get to know the business, or for my own use), **I want** to record them from my phone in a few taps (date, varieties and grams, the reason), **so I can** keep stock right and see in Ganancia what promoting the business and my own consumption cost me, without recording a sale nobody made or an adjustment that reads as lost flower.

It refines BRIEF.md's stock job ("stock per variety stays right") and its results job ("whether I make money"). It is not a new top-level job.

**Q3**: Accepted, with two additions so the criteria cover the two distortions this feature exists to end. The success criteria are:
1. **Fast from the phone**: a withdrawal is recorded in under a minute, and the form opens in at most 2 taps from any tab root.
2. **Stock stays right**: once recorded, edited or deleted, a withdrawal shows at once in the variety's stock, status, days left and history, and in the lots after it. Pending grams are pending, never $0.
3. **Its own lines in Ganancia**: Inicio's Semana and Mes and Análisis show "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" as separate lines at lot cost, each lowering Ganancia on its own date, with pending marked the same way as today. They are never folded into cost of goods sold or stock shortfall.
4. **Not a sale, not a loss**: sales count, sales total, average sale, Ventas, every customer's history and Caja stay exactly as they were. Stock shortfall never includes a withdrawal.

"This month's promotion cost at a glance" is covered by criterion 3, since Mes shows the line. Nothing about who attended or about later sales (out of scope).

**Q4**: Accepted as the problem statement. Today the seller either distorts the books (a sale without a customer made of gift lines, which inflates sales count and average sale, or a negative adjustment, which reads as missing flower and counts as stock shortfall) or doesn't record the grams, so stock drifts until the next count corrects it as shortfall. Assumption: the "doesn't record" case happens. It is just the third version of the same distortion and changes nothing in the PRD.

### Round 2: questions

### Round 2: behaviour

The job is settled and I won't reopen it. These questions are about behaviour that serves it. Product knowledge already settles some points, so I don't ask about them:
- Recording is never refused for lack of stock (ADR-0009, stock.md "Overselling"). A withdrawal follows that rule.
- Withdrawals take from the oldest lots first, and the part beyond every lot is pending cost, never $0 (CONTEXT.md: Lot, Pending cost).
- Caja is untouched, because no money moves.

❓ **Q1** - **Shape of a withdrawal**: Your request says "a date, one or more varieties with their grams, and an optional note", for one reason: promotion or own use. I read that as the reason being set once for the whole withdrawal, not per variety line. A party that mixes promotion and own use would be two withdrawals. Is that right? The other rules I'd write, copied from stock entries and sales: grams above zero with at most two decimals; the same variety may appear in several lines; the date defaults to today and can't be in the future; the note is optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters, and never logged.
➡️ Recommendation: One reason per withdrawal, several variety lines, and the rules above. It is simpler to record on the phone, and Ganancia only needs the reason to put grams on the right line.

❓ **Q2** - **Where Promoción and Consumo propio show in the results**: Inicio's Hoy shows no costs at all (results.md). I propose these rules:
- **Inicio**: Semana and Mes show "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" as separate lines, each only when the period has a withdrawal of that reason, like "Faltante de stock". Hoy shows neither.
- **Análisis**: the same two lines appear in the chosen month's header, in the week and month rows, and in the since-the-first-record totals. They sit separate from "Faltante de stock".
- **Pending cost**: each line carries its own "y N g sin costo" in Análisis and the fixed "incluye costo pendiente" note on Inicio. Ganancia carries the sum of every pending gram behind it.
- **Ganancia**: it subtracts both lines on each withdrawal's own date. When any of its cost is pending, it is worked out over the costed part, as today.

Do you agree? If not, which part changes?
➡️ Recommendation: Yes, as proposed. It mirrors Faltante de stock, which is the closest existing figure, and it matches your note that they are "never folded into cost of goods sold".

❓ **Q3** - **Where it is recorded and where it is seen afterwards**: Your request says the form opens from Más and from a variety's page. I propose these rules:
- **Más**: "Sacar del stock" sits next to "+ Cargar gasto". With the entry point on a variety's page, the form opens with that variety already chosen. It is a form: "Cerrar", and a confirmation when leaving with something typed.
- **Tap budget**: at most 2 taps from any tab root, as for stock entries. On a computer, no more taps than on the phone, with no Más. The route is the one the computer needs; I leave the exact place to design and navigation.
- **History**: a variety's page shows its withdrawals in "Stock y entradas", told apart from entries by the words "Promoción" and "Consumo propio". Its "Ver todas" holds them too. "Todas las entradas" (the business-wide list) lists only entries, as today.

Is that right, or do you want a business-wide list of withdrawals as well?
➡️ Recommendation: As proposed, with no business-wide list. Your request asks only for them to show in the variety's history, and a second list would be a second place for the same fact (navigation.md, criterion 3).

❓ **Q4** - **Margins by variety**: In Análisis, the chosen month's table shows each variety's sales, grams, cost, margin and margin percentage. A negative adjustment is kept out of every variety's grams, cost and margin. A promotion or own-use withdrawal isn't a sale or a gift, so I propose the same: it never enters a variety's table, grams sold or gifted, margin, share of the month or best marks, and it shows only in the two new lines. The days-of-stock rate does count withdrawn grams, as your request says.

Do you agree?
➡️ Recommendation: Yes. A variety's margin stays about what customers bought, and the cost of promotion and own use is a business-level figure.

❓ **Q5** - **Edit, delete and feedback**: Your request says a withdrawal may be edited or deleted at any time, and the lots after it are worked out again. I propose these rules:
- **Edit**: every field can change (date, reason, lines, note) with the same validation as recording. Saving is confirmed first, like a stock entry.
- **Delete**: a hard delete, confirmed with "Antes de borrar", naming the date, reason and total grams, e.g. "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." The grams return to stock and land back on lots by date.
- **Toasts**: "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no grams or amounts.
- **Gone record**: it follows accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule, with the way back to Variedades.
- **Catalog**: a withdrawal changes stock, so it is a trigger for the pinned catalog's upkeep, like a stock entry or a sale.

Do you agree? Also, is "Retiro" the word you want on screen in the toasts and confirmations, next to "Sacar del stock" as the button?
➡️ Recommendation: Yes, as proposed, using "Retiro" for the record. Edit, delete, toasts and upkeep copy what stock entries and sales already do, so nothing new needs learning.

### Round 2: answers

### Round 2: behaviour

**Q1**: Accepted. A withdrawal has one reason, Promoción or Consumo propio, and one or more lines of variety and grams. A party that mixes both reasons is two withdrawals. The rules are the ones you listed:
- grams above zero, at most two decimals;
- the same variety may appear in several lines, with no merging;
- the date defaults to today and can't be in the future;
- the note is optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters, and never logged, searched or repeated in errors.

Two more rules, copied from sales and stock entries:
- A withdrawal with no lines is refused.
- A variety from another business answers 404.

Every refusal carries its code in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) and never repeats the input (ADR-0005).

**Q2**: Accepted. Two things to add:
- **They match Análisis exactly.** Semana's and Mes's Promoción and Consumo propio equal Análisis's figures for the same week and month, with 0 differences. They come from the same single period computation, so there is no second sum (ADR-0020, ADR-0013 amendment). This joins results.md criterion 2.
- **Pending grams.** Ganancia's "y N g sin costo" adds the pending grams sold, gifted, taken by negative adjustments and withdrawn, each counted in the period of its own date. Each new line's N counts only its own reason's grams.

**Q3**: Accepted, with one change for the computer.
- **On the phone**: "Sacar del stock" goes in Más, second, right after "+ Cargar gasto". It is the other entry with no shorter path.
- **On a variety's page**: a "Sacar del stock" action sits next to "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio", and opens the form with that variety already chosen. How the three actions sit together is design's call.
- **On a computer**: there is no Más, and opening a variety's page first would break "no more taps than on the phone". So Variedades gets a secondary "Sacar del stock" next to "+ Entrada de stock", on both phone and computer. Stock in and stock out then sit together, and the tap budget holds everywhere: at most 2 taps from any tab root.
- **The form**: it is a form under the navigation rule ("Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", a confirmation when leaving with something typed). It follows the phone standard for inputs and targets (#80).
- **History**: a withdrawal shows in "Stock y entradas" and its "Ver todas" on the page of each variety it touches. It is told apart from entries by the words "Promoción" or "Consumo propio" and its grams shown as going out. Opening it shows the whole withdrawal, with every line, to edit or delete.
- **No business-wide list.** "Todas las entradas" stays entries only.

**Q4**: Accepted. A withdrawal never enters the variety table, grams sold or gifted, margin, margin percentage, share of the month, average margin, best week or best weekday. It shows only in the two new lines and in Ganancia. On the stock side:
- it counts as grams going out for days left;
- it counts as movement for the "no entries" status;
- it is a movement for "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm";
- it may push stock negative, shown as sold out, like any draw.

**Q5**: Accepted, with "Retiro" as the record's word on screen. "Sacar del stock" is the action, "Retiro" is the record, and the reason is named where it helps:
- the toasts are "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no data;
- the delete confirmation names the reason, as in your example: "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." / "¿Borrás el consumo propio del 03/10? …", with "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo".

The rest stays as you proposed:
- an edit is confirmed before saving;
- a delete is a hard delete, and the grams land back on lots by date;
- a withdrawal that is gone follows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades";
- recording, editing and deleting a withdrawal are upkeep triggers for the pinned catalog.

Assumption: CONTEXT.md gets "Retiro" as the In-the-app word for Stock withdrawal, next to the action "Sacar del stock". The routes join the isolation sweep, the gated-route test and the ownership migration test. Business removal and its export cover the new tables, which ADR-0015's schema reflection already does.

### Closing

### Closing summary

Nothing is left that could change the PRD. Round 2 settled every behaviour question, so I'm closing at round 3.

**Job (settled earlier, not reopened).** The owner takes grams out of stock for promotion or own use and wants the stock and the profit to stay true. No money moves.

**Decided**
- **Shape.** A Retiro has one reason (Promoción or Consumo propio), a date and one or more lines of variety and grams.
  - Grams are above zero with at most two decimals. The same variety may appear in several lines.
  - The date defaults to today and can't be in the future.
  - The note is optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters, and never logged, searched or repeated in errors.
  - A withdrawal with no lines is refused. A variety from another business answers 404.
  - Refusals carry their codes in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) and never repeat the input (ADR-0005).
  - A party that mixes both reasons is two withdrawals.
- **Stock and cost.** A withdrawal is never refused for lack of stock (ADR-0009). It takes from the oldest lots first, and any part beyond every lot is pending cost, never $0. Stock may go negative and shows as sold out. Caja is untouched.
- **Results.**
  - Inicio's Semana and Mes show "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X", each only when the period has one. Hoy shows neither.
  - Análisis shows both lines in the month header, the week and month rows and the since-first-record totals, separate from Faltante de stock.
  - Inicio and Análisis match exactly, because they come from one period computation (ADR-0020, ADR-0013 amendment).
  - Pending grams: each line carries its own "y N g sin costo", counting only its own reason's grams. Ganancia's count adds the pending grams sold, gifted, negatively adjusted and withdrawn, each in the period of its own date.
  - Ganancia subtracts both lines on each withdrawal's own date.
- **Varieties.** A withdrawal never enters a variety's table, grams sold or gifted, margin, share of the month, average margin or best marks.
  - It counts as grams going out for days of stock left.
  - It counts as movement for the "no entries" status and for "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm".
- **Where.**
  - "Sacar del stock" sits in Más, second, right after "+ Cargar gasto".
  - On a variety's page it sits next to "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio". The form opens with that variety chosen.
  - Variedades gets a secondary "Sacar del stock" next to "+ Entrada de stock", on phone and computer.
  - The tap budget is at most 2 taps from any tab root.
  - The form follows the navigation rule: "Cerrar", no bar, and a confirmation when leaving with something typed. It follows the phone standard (#80).
  - History shows in "Stock y entradas" and "Ver todas" on each variety touched, marked by the reason and its grams going out. Opening one shows all its lines, to edit or delete.
  - There is no business-wide list. "Todas las entradas" stays entries only.
- **Edit and delete.**
  - Every field can be edited, and saving is confirmed first.
  - Delete is a hard delete. The confirmation names the reason, e.g. "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock.", with "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo". The grams land back on lots by date.
  - The toasts are "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no data.
  - A gone record shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
  - Recording, editing and deleting are upkeep triggers for the pinned catalog.
- **Cross-cutting.** The new routes join the isolation sweep, the gated-route test and the ownership migration test. Business removal and its export cover the new tables, which ADR-0015's schema reflection already does.

**Recorded.** CONTEXT.md now defines **Stock withdrawal** (in the app: Retiro, action "Sacar del stock", reasons Promoción and Consumo propio). No new ADR was needed.

**Nothing escalated to the human.**

## Product Analyst

### Problem

Grams leave a business's stock without anyone buying them. The seller takes a few grams to a party so several people can try them and get to know the business, or consumes some personally. Today the app only offers two ways to record that, and both distort the books:

- **A sale without a customer made of gift lines.** It counts a sale nobody made, and inflates the sales count, the average sale and Ventas.
- **A negative adjustment.** It reads as missing flower and lands in Faltante de stock.

Some grams are never recorded at all. Stock then drifts until the next count, and the count corrects it as shortfall, which is the same distortion. Ganancia has no line for what promotion and own use cost.

### Job to be done

**When** I take grams out of my own stock without selling them (to share at a party so people get to know the business, or for my own use), **I want** to record them from my phone in a few taps (date, varieties and grams, the reason), **so I can** keep stock right and see in Ganancia what promoting the business and my own consumption cost me, without recording a sale nobody made or an adjustment that reads as lost flower.

This refines BRIEF.md's stock job and its results job. It is not a new top-level job. The withdrawal records grams that have already left stock, dated the day they left. A seller catching up later sets an earlier date. There are no planned or draft withdrawals.

### Success criteria

1. **Fast from the phone.** A withdrawal is recorded in under a minute. The form opens in at most 2 taps from any tab root. On a computer it takes no more taps than on the phone.
2. **Stock stays right.** Once a withdrawal is recorded, edited or deleted, the change shows at once in the variety's stock, status, days left and history, and in the lots after it. Pending grams are pending, never $0.
3. **Its own lines in Ganancia.** Inicio's Semana and Mes and Análisis show "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" as separate lines at lot cost.
   - Each lowers Ganancia on its own date.
   - Pending cost is marked as it is today.
   - Neither is ever folded into Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
4. **Not a sale, not a loss.** Sales count, sales total, average sale, Ventas, every customer's history and Caja stay exactly as they were. Faltante de stock never includes a withdrawal.
5. **One computation.** Semana's and Mes's Promoción, Consumo propio and Ganancia equal Análisis's figures for the same week and month, with 0 differences (ADR-0020, ADR-0013 amendment).
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

### Behaviour

#### Glossary (CONTEXT.md)

- **Stock withdrawal** is already defined there. In the app: Retiro, recorded with the action "Sacar del stock".
- The same change adds **Promotion** (Promoción) and **Own use** (Consumo propio) as terms.
  - Promotion is a withdrawal to make the business known. Avoid: gift, sample, freebie.
  - Own use is a withdrawal the seller consumes personally. Avoid: personal, shrinkage.
- **Gift** gains the line "Grams shared to promote the business are a promotion, never a gift." and "promo" leaves its Avoid list.
- **Draw** includes a stock withdrawal.
- **Stock** is everything that came in minus everything sold, gifted or withdrawn.
- **Profit** is sales − cost of goods sold − stock shortfall − promotion cost − own-use cost − expenses.
  - The two new costs are what the withdrawn grams cost from their lots as they stand today.
  - Pending grams join the "y N g sin costo" count.
  - Caja is unaffected.
- **Days left** and **Stock status** ("no entries") count a withdrawal as grams going out and as movement.

#### The withdrawal

- **Fields**: one reason (Promoción or Consumo propio), a date, one or more lines of variety and grams, and an optional note.
- **Mixed reasons**: a party that mixes both reasons is two withdrawals.
- **Reason**: set once for the whole withdrawal, never per line.
- **Date**: defaults to today in Argentina time. Earlier days are allowed. A future date is refused.
- **Lines**: grams above zero with at most two decimals. The same variety may appear in several lines, with no merging. A withdrawal with no lines is refused.
- **Variety**: must belong to the business. Any other id answers 404.
- **Note**: optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters. Empty means none. It is never logged, searched or repeated in errors.
- **Refusals**: each carries a code in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) and never repeats the input (ADR-0005).
- **Stock**: recording is never refused for lack of stock (ADR-0009). Stock may go negative and then shows as sold out.
- **Lots and cost**: the grams take from the oldest lots of their variety first, in lot order. A part beyond every lot is pending cost, never $0. A later stock entry settles it, as for any draw.
- **No money**: no money moves and Caja is untouched.
- **Edit**: every field can change, with the same validation as recording. Saving is confirmed first. The lots after it are worked out again.
- **Delete**: a hard delete, confirmed with "Antes de borrar", naming the reason, date and total grams. For example: "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." or "¿Borrás el consumo propio del 03/10? …". The buttons are "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo". The grams return to stock and land back on lots by date.
- **Never refused**: neither edit nor delete is refused because the withdrawal's grams were already drawn from lots (ADR-0009).

#### Results

- **Inicio**
  - Semana and Mes show "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" as separate lines, each only when the period has a withdrawal of that reason, like "Faltante de stock".
  - Hoy shows neither.
  - The fixed "incluye costo pendiente" note appears under each new line when it has pending grams, and under Ganancia when any of its cost is pending.
- **Análisis**
  - The two lines appear in the chosen month's header, in the week and month rows, and in the since-the-first-record totals.
  - They sit apart from "Faltante de stock".
  - Each carries its own "y N g sin costo", counting only its own reason's grams.
- **Ganancia**
  - It subtracts both lines on each withdrawal's own date.
  - When any of its cost is pending, it is worked out over the costed part, as today.
  - Its "y N g sin costo" adds the pending grams sold, gifted, taken by negative adjustments and withdrawn, each counted in the period of its own date.
- **Single computation**: every period figure comes from the one period computation Análisis and Inicio already share. There is no second sum.
- **Varieties**: a withdrawal never enters a variety's table, grams sold or gifted, margin, margin percentage, share of the month, average margin, best week or best weekday.
- **Stock side**
  - It counts as grams going out for days left.
  - It counts as movement for the "no entries" status.
  - It counts as a movement for "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm".

#### Where it is recorded and seen

- **Más (phone)**: "Sacar del stock" sits second, right after "+ Cargar gasto". It opens the form with today's date.
- **A variety's page**: a "Sacar del stock" action sits next to "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio", and opens the form with that variety already chosen. How the three actions sit together is design's call.
- **Variedades (phone and computer)**: a secondary "Sacar del stock" sits next to "+ Entrada de stock". It gives the computer, which has no Más, the same tap budget as the phone.
- **The form**
  - It is a form under the navigation rule: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", and a confirmation when leaving with something typed.
  - It follows the phone standard for inputs and targets (#80).
- **History**
  - A withdrawal shows in "Stock y entradas" and "Ver todas" on the page of each variety it touches.
  - It is told apart from entries by the words "Promoción" or "Consumo propio", with its grams shown as going out.
  - Opening it shows the whole withdrawal, with every line, to edit or delete.
- **No business-wide list.** "Todas las entradas" stays entries only.
- **Navigation docs**: the structure (navigation.md, `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/`, `scripts/design/screens.json`) states the new entries and routes. Routes are in English; the exact paths are fixed there. Routes that share a first segment with the API follow ADR-0024.

#### Feedback

- **Toasts**: "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no grams or amounts.
- **Refusals**: they show inline and keep what was typed.
- **A withdrawal that is gone**, following accounts.md:
  - Opening it shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
  - Saving, deleting or confirming on it shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
- **Pinned catalog**: recording, editing and deleting a withdrawal are upkeep triggers, like a stock entry (pinned-catalog.md).

#### Constraints

- Every new table carries `business_id`, and the business comes from the session.
- Every new route joins the isolation sweep, the gated-route test and the ownership migration test.
- Business removal and its export cover the new tables through the schema reflection of ADR-0015.
- No note, grams or amount goes to logs, the console or browser storage.
- Copy is in Rioplatense Spanish with vos.

### Metrics

- **Record time (criterion 1)**
  - Through the front, from a phone viewport, a withdrawal is recorded in under 60 s.
  - The form opens in at most 2 taps from every tab root, on phone and computer viewports.
  - The tap count on a computer is never above the phone's.
- **Stock and lots (criterion 2)**, 100% passing:
  - after record, edit and delete, stock equals in minus out, to the hundredth;
  - editing or deleting a withdrawal works the lots after it out again, and moves only that variety's costs;
  - pending grams are never costed $0;
  - a withdrawal past zero stock is recorded and shows sold out.
- **Ganancia lines (criteria 3, 5)**, 100% passing:
  - with a seeded history that holds a costed withdrawal of each reason, a withdrawal with pending grams, a negative adjustment and a sale line with pending grams, Inicio's Semana and Mes equal Análisis's row for the same week and month, with 0 differences;
  - each line lowers Ganancia on its own date;
  - neither enters Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock;
  - a period with no withdrawal of a reason shows no line for it;
  - Hoy shows neither;
  - each line's pending count counts only its own reason, and Ganancia's count adds every pending gram behind it.
- **Not a sale, not a loss (criterion 4)**: after recording a withdrawal, 0 differences in:
  - sales total and count;
  - average sale;
  - Ventas;
  - every customer's balance and account;
  - Caja;
  - Faltante de stock;
  - every variety's margin and grams sold or gifted.
- **Stock side**: days left counts withdrawn grams. A variety with only a withdrawal is not "sin entradas". "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm" follows the withdrawal's date.
- **Validation matrix**: grams at or below zero, more than two decimals, a future date, a missing reason, no lines, a note over 200 characters and another business's variety each give their own coded refusal, which never repeats the input.
- **Isolation (criterion 6)**: every withdrawal route is in the sweep and answers 404 or empty from another business, including a reference to another business's variety.
- **No echo**: no refusal body or log line contains the note, grams or amounts. After a full front run, the console and browser storage hold none of them.
- **Front tests**:
  - the form has the form chrome and asks before discarding;
  - the confirmations, toasts and "Eso ya no está." forms show their fixed text;
  - the history rows are told apart by word;
  - there is no business-wide list.
- **Upkeep**: a withdrawal that changes whether a variety qualifies for the catalog triggers an edit of the pinned message. One that does not change the text sends nothing.
- **No curl**: the end-to-end front run records a withdrawal from Más and from a variety's page, edits and deletes one, and reads Promoción and Consumo propio on Inicio and Análisis.
- **Looks as designed**: before and after comparisons from `scripts/design/compare.sh`, phone and computer, judged by eye.

### Out of scope

- Who attended a party or how many tried.
- Tying a promotion to later sales.
- Money spent on a party. That is an expense.
- A business-wide list of withdrawals.
- A per-variety breakdown of promotion or own-use cost.
- Planned or draft withdrawals.
- Splitting one withdrawal across two reasons.
- Live updates between partners.

### Dependencies

The screens follow the new navigation (#58/#59) and the phone standard (#80). The backend and glossary can land first.

## PM (v1)

### Verdict

**approved**: The PRD follows every decision from both rounds and serves the settled job. I closed the small gaps myself; none of them blocks building.

### Adjustments

1. **What opening a withdrawal shows.** The PRD said "opening it shows the whole withdrawal" but did not say what kind of screen that is. It is now a **detail** under navigation.md's rule:
   - It shows the bar or sidebar and a "‹" back to the variety's page it was opened from. With no history, back goes to Variedades. It never shows "+ Nueva venta".
   - It shows the date, the reason, every line (variety and grams) and the note.
   - Its actions are "Editar" and "Borrar". "Editar" opens the same "Sacar del stock" form, already filled in.
2. **History rows.** On a variety's page, a withdrawal row shows:
   - the date;
   - the reason by word ("Promoción" or "Consumo propio");
   - that variety's grams in the withdrawal with a minus sign ("−5 g"). Going out is told by sign and word, never by color.

   Rows mix with stock entries, newest date first, then most recently recorded first.
3. **Reason has no default.** The PRD lists "a missing reason" as a refusal. The form therefore asks for the reason every time (Promoción · Consumo propio), like the expense category.
4. **Where the lines sit on Inicio.** Promoción and Consumo propio sit right after Faltante de stock and before Gastos, in Semana and Mes.
5. **Glossary knock-ons the PRD left out.** Lot, Pending cost and Panel also change: withdrawn grams come out of lots and can be pending, and the panel gains the two lines.
6. **Lot order.** A withdrawal line is a draw in the existing lot order: by date, then recording order. How it ties with a sale line or a negative adjustment on the exact same instant is the Tech Lead's call under ADR-0010. It is not a product rule.
7. **Knock-ons in other capability files**, consolidated in the definitions:
   - navigation.md: Más's entry list, the tap budget, and the chrome lists (the form, and the withdrawal's detail);
   - varieties.md: the page's actions and the list's secondary button;
   - pinned-catalog.md: the upkeep trigger list;
   - results.md and analysis.md: the figures and formulas.

### Validated flags

- `touches_ui`: **true**. A new form, a new detail, new entries in Más, Variedades and a variety's page, and new lines on Inicio and Análisis.
- `touches_copy`: **true**. "Sacar del stock", "Retiro guardado"/"Retiro eliminado", the delete confirmation, and the "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" lines.

### PRD

#### Problem

Grams leave a business's stock without anyone buying them. The seller takes a few grams to a party so several people can try them and get to know the business, or consumes some personally. Today the app only offers two ways to record that, and both distort the books:

- **A sale without a customer made of gift lines.** It counts a sale nobody made, and inflates the sales count, the average sale and Ventas.
- **A negative adjustment.** It reads as missing flower and lands in Faltante de stock.

Some grams are never recorded at all. Stock then drifts until the next count, and the count corrects it as shortfall, which is the same distortion. Ganancia has no line for what promotion and own use cost.

#### Job to be done

**When** I take grams out of my own stock without selling them (to share at a party so people get to know the business, or for my own use), **I want** to record them from my phone in a few taps (date, varieties and grams, the reason), **so I can** keep stock right and see in Ganancia what promoting the business and my own consumption cost me, without recording a sale nobody made or an adjustment that reads as lost flower.

This refines BRIEF.md's stock job and its results job. It is not a new top-level job.

- A withdrawal records grams that have already left stock, dated the day they left.
- A seller catching up later sets an earlier date.
- There are no planned or draft withdrawals.

#### Success criteria

1. **Fast from the phone.** A withdrawal is recorded in under a minute. The form opens in at most 2 taps from any tab root. On a computer it takes no more taps than on the phone.
2. **Stock stays right.** Once a withdrawal is recorded, edited or deleted, the change shows at once in the variety's stock, status, days left and history, and in the lots after it. Pending grams are pending, never $0.
3. **Its own lines in Ganancia.** Inicio's Semana and Mes and Análisis show "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" as separate lines at lot cost.
   - Each lowers Ganancia on its own date.
   - Pending cost is marked as it is today.
   - Neither is ever folded into Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
4. **Not a sale, not a loss.** These stay exactly as they were:
   - sales count, sales total and average sale;
   - Ventas;
   - every customer's history;
   - Caja.

   Faltante de stock never includes a withdrawal.
5. **One computation.** Semana's and Mes's Promoción, Consumo propio and Ganancia equal Análisis's figures for the same week and month, with 0 differences (ADR-0020, ADR-0013 amendment).
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

#### Behaviour

##### Glossary (CONTEXT.md)

- **Stock withdrawal** is already defined there. In the app it is a Retiro, recorded with the action "Sacar del stock".
- **New terms**:
  - **Promotion** (Promoción): a withdrawal to make the business known. Avoid: gift, sample, freebie.
  - **Own use** (Consumo propio): a withdrawal the seller consumes personally. Avoid: personal, shrinkage.
- **Gift** gains the line "Grams shared to promote the business are a promotion, never a gift.", and "promo" leaves its Avoid list.
- **Draw**, **Lot** and **Pending cost** include withdrawn grams.
- **Stock** is everything that came in minus everything sold, gifted or withdrawn.
- **Profit** is sales − cost of goods sold − stock shortfall − promotion cost − own-use cost − expenses.
  - The two new costs are what the withdrawn grams cost, from their lots as they stand today.
  - Pending grams join the "y N g sin costo" count.
  - Caja is unaffected.
- **Days left** and **Stock status** ("no entries") count a withdrawal as grams going out and as movement.
- **Panel**: Semana and Mes gain the two lines.

##### The withdrawal

- **Fields**: one reason (Promoción or Consumo propio), a date, one or more lines of variety and grams, and an optional note.
- **Reason**:
  - set once for the whole withdrawal, never per line;
  - required, with no default: it is chosen every time;
  - a party that mixes both reasons is two withdrawals.
- **Date**: defaults to today in Argentina time. Earlier days are allowed. A future date is refused.
- **Lines**:
  - grams above zero, with at most two decimals;
  - the same variety may appear in several lines, with no merging;
  - a withdrawal with no lines is refused.
- **Variety**: must belong to the business. Any other id answers 404.
- **Note**: optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters. Empty means none. It is never logged, searched or repeated in errors.
- **Refusals**: each carries a code in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) and never repeats the input (ADR-0005).
- **Stock**: recording is never refused for lack of stock (ADR-0009). Stock may go negative and then shows as sold out.
- **Lots and cost**:
  - each line is a draw, taking from the oldest lots of its variety first, in lot order (by date, then recording order);
  - a part beyond every lot is pending cost, never $0;
  - a later stock entry settles the pending part, as for any draw.
- **No money moves**: Caja is untouched.
- **Edit**:
  - every field can change, with the same validation as recording;
  - saving is confirmed first;
  - the lots after it are worked out again.
- **Delete**:
  - a hard delete, confirmed with "Antes de borrar", naming the reason, the date and the total grams;
  - for example "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." or "¿Borrás el consumo propio del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock.";
  - the buttons are "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo";
  - the grams return to stock and land back on lots by date.
- **Never refused because of lots**: neither an edit nor a delete is refused because the withdrawal's grams were already drawn from lots (ADR-0009).

##### Results

- **Inicio**:
  - Semana and Mes show "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" as separate lines, each only when the period has a withdrawal of that reason, like "Faltante de stock";
  - they sit right after Faltante de stock and before Gastos;
  - Hoy shows neither;
  - the fixed "incluye costo pendiente" note appears under each new line when it has pending grams, and under Ganancia when any of its cost is pending.
- **Análisis**:
  - the two lines appear in the chosen month's header, in the week and month rows, and in the since-the-first-record totals;
  - they sit apart from "Faltante de stock";
  - each carries its own "y N g sin costo", counting only its own reason's grams.
- **Ganancia**:
  - it subtracts both lines on each withdrawal's own date;
  - when any of its cost is pending, it is worked out over the costed part, as today;
  - its "y N g sin costo" adds the pending grams sold, gifted, taken by negative adjustments and withdrawn, each counted in the period of its own date.
- **One computation**: every period figure comes from the one period computation Análisis and Inicio already share. There is no second sum.
- **Varieties**: a withdrawal never enters any of these:
  - a variety's table;
  - grams sold or gifted;
  - margin, margin percentage or share of the month;
  - average margin;
  - best week or best weekday.
- **Stock side**: a withdrawal counts
  - as grams going out for days left;
  - as movement for the "no entries" status;
  - as a movement for "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm".

##### Where it is recorded and seen

- **Más (phone)**: "Sacar del stock" sits second, right after "+ Cargar gasto". It opens the form with today's date.
- **A variety's page**: a "Sacar del stock" action sits next to "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio", and opens the form with that variety already chosen. How the three actions sit together is design's call.
- **Variedades (phone and computer)**: a secondary "Sacar del stock" sits next to "+ Entrada de stock". It gives the computer, which has no Más, the same tap budget as the phone.
- **The form**:
  - it is a form under the navigation rule: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", and a confirmation when leaving with something typed;
  - it follows the phone standard for inputs and targets (#80).
- **History**:
  - A withdrawal shows in "Stock y entradas" and its "Ver todas" on the page of each variety it touches.
  - Each row shows the date, "Promoción" or "Consumo propio", and that variety's grams with a minus sign (e.g. "−5 g").
  - Rows mix with stock entries, newest date first, then most recently recorded first.
  - Going out is told by word and sign, never by color.
- **A withdrawal's detail**:
  - It is a detail, with the bar or sidebar and a "‹" back to the variety's page it was opened from. With no history, back goes to Variedades. It shows no "+ Nueva venta".
  - It shows the date, the reason, every line and the note.
  - "Editar" opens the same form, already filled in. "Borrar" asks the confirmation above.
- **No business-wide list.** "Todas las entradas" stays entries only.
- **Navigation docs**:
  - The structure (navigation.md, `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/`, `scripts/design/screens.json`) states the new entries and routes.
  - Routes are in English, and the exact paths are fixed there.
  - A route that shares a first segment with the API follows ADR-0024.

##### Feedback

- **Toasts**: "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no grams or amounts.
- **Refusals** show inline and keep what was typed.
- **A withdrawal that is gone**, following accounts.md:
  - opening it shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades";
  - saving, deleting or confirming on it shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
- **Pinned catalog**: recording, editing and deleting a withdrawal are upkeep triggers, like a stock entry (pinned-catalog.md).

##### Constraints

- Every new table carries `business_id`, and the business comes from the session.
- Every new route joins the isolation sweep, the gated-route test and the ownership migration test.
- Business removal and its export cover the new tables through ADR-0015's schema reflection.
- No note, grams or amount goes to logs, the console or browser storage.
- Copy is in Rioplatense Spanish with vos.

#### Metrics

- **Record time (criterion 1)**:
  - through the front, from a phone viewport, a withdrawal is recorded in under 60 s;
  - the form opens in at most 2 taps from every tab root, on phone and computer viewports;
  - the tap count on a computer is never above the phone's.
- **Stock and lots (criterion 2)**, 100% passing:
  - after a record, an edit and a delete, stock equals in minus out, to the hundredth;
  - editing or deleting a withdrawal works the lots after it out again, and moves only that variety's costs;
  - pending grams are never costed $0;
  - a withdrawal past zero stock is recorded and shows sold out.
- **Ganancia lines (criteria 3, 5)**, 100% passing. The seeded history holds a costed withdrawal of each reason, a withdrawal with pending grams, a negative adjustment and a sale line with pending grams.
  - Inicio's Semana and Mes equal Análisis's row for the same week and month, with 0 differences.
  - Each line lowers Ganancia on its own date.
  - Neither enters Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
  - A period with no withdrawal of a reason shows no line for it.
  - Hoy shows neither line.
  - Each line's pending count counts only its own reason, and Ganancia's count adds every pending gram behind it.
- **Not a sale, not a loss (criterion 4)**: after recording a withdrawal, 0 differences in:
  - sales total and count;
  - average sale;
  - Ventas;
  - every customer's balance and account;
  - Caja;
  - Faltante de stock;
  - every variety's margin and grams sold or gifted.
- **Stock side**:
  - days left counts withdrawn grams;
  - a variety with only a withdrawal is not "sin entradas";
  - "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm" follows the withdrawal's date.
- **Validation matrix**: each of these gives its own coded refusal, which never repeats the input:
  - grams at or below zero;
  - more than two decimals;
  - a future date;
  - a missing reason;
  - no lines;
  - a note over 200 characters;
  - another business's variety.
- **Isolation (criterion 6)**: every withdrawal route is in the sweep and answers 404 or empty from another business, including a reference to another business's variety.
- **No echo**:
  - no refusal body or log line contains the note, grams or amounts;
  - after a full front run, the console and browser storage hold none of them.
- **Front tests**:
  - the form has the form chrome and asks before discarding;
  - the detail has the detail chrome;
  - the confirmations, the toasts and both "Eso ya no está." forms show their fixed text;
  - the history rows are told apart by word and sign;
  - there is no business-wide list.
- **Upkeep**:
  - a withdrawal that changes whether a variety qualifies for the catalog triggers an edit of the pinned message;
  - one that does not change the text sends nothing.
- **No curl**: the end-to-end front run records a withdrawal from Más and from a variety's page, edits and deletes one, and reads Promoción and Consumo propio on Inicio and Análisis.
- **Looks as designed**: before and after comparisons from `scripts/design/compare.sh`, on phone and computer, judged by eye.

#### Out of scope

- Who attended a party, or how many tried.
- Tying a promotion to later sales.
- Money spent on a party. That is an expense.
- A business-wide list of withdrawals.
- A per-variety breakdown of promotion or own-use cost.
- Planned or draft withdrawals.
- Splitting one withdrawal across two reasons.
- Live updates between partners.

#### Dependencies

The screens follow the new navigation (#58/#59) and the phone standard (#80). The backend and glossary can land first.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:

- **Cost goes through the existing draw pipeline.** Each withdrawal line is a draw in stock's lot order. Withdrawals reach `allocate` through the same `lots_and_draws` load and the draw-row builder that `SqlDrawCosts` shares (ADR-0010, ADR-0013 amendment).
  - They need a new draw kind beside `sold`, `gifted` and `adjustment`, split by reason.
  - Where a withdrawal sits on an exact tie with a sale line or a negative adjustment is your call. Record it in an ADR if it changes ADR-0010's `LotOrder`.
- **Period figures stay one sum.** Promoción and Consumo propio come from ADR-0020's `scan` and `Buckets`. Inicio and Análisis read the same `PeriodTotals`, and profit's formula is written once.
  - Each new line answers its own pending flag on Inicio and its own pending grams on Análisis.
  - Ganancia's pending count adds the withdrawn grams.
  - Margins, variety rows, grams sold or gifted, best week and best weekday must not see withdrawals.
- **Stock's own figures.** `stock_level` counts withdrawals for stock, status ("no entries", "Sin gramos desde"), days left and pending grams. Total pending still equals `max(0, −stock)`.
- **Mind the module boundary.** Whether withdrawals live in `app/stock/` or a new hexagon is yours. Sales, Ventas, payments and Caja must not read them.
- **Upkeep.** The withdrawal routers trigger upkeep after commit, as stock entries do (ADR-0014). pinned-catalog.md's trigger list is updated.
- **Navigation and routes.**
  - Add the form and the detail to `scripts/design/screens.json`.
  - Add "Sacar del stock" second in Más, on Variedades and on a variety's page.
  - Follow ADR-0024 if the front path shares a segment with the API.
  - New sheets use `useSheet` (ADR-0027). Phone inputs follow #80 (44 px targets, 16 px inputs, decimal keypad for grams).
- **Business removal.**
  - ADR-0015's reflection covers the new tables. The domain may need a declared label for a table without `name`.
  - accounts.md's export list and dry-run kinds will name the new kinds when they are next consolidated. Reflection already exports and deletes them.
- **Tests.** The isolation sweep, gated routes and ownership migration test, plus refusal codes in `contract.json` for every validation-matrix case.
