2. **Stock stays right.** Once a withdrawal is recorded, edited or deleted, the change shows at once in the variety's stock, status, days left and history, and in the lots after it. Pending grams are pending, never $0.
3. **Its own lines in Ganancia.** Inicio's Semana and Mes and Análisis show "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" as separate lines at lot cost.
   - Each lowers Ganancia on its own date.
   - Pending cost is marked as it is today.
   - Neither is ever folded into Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
4. **Not a sale, not a loss.** These stay exactly as they were:
   - sales count, sales total and average sale;
   - Ventas;
   - every customer's history;
   - Caja.

   Faltante de stock never includes a withdrawal.
5. **One computation.** Semana's and Mes's Promoción, Consumo propio and Ganancia equal Análisis's figures for the same week and month, with 0 differences (ADR-0020, ADR-0013 amendment).
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

#### Behaviour

##### Glossary (CONTEXT.md)

- **Stock withdrawal** is already defined there. In the app it is a Retiro, recorded with the action "Sacar del stock".
- **New terms**:
  - **Promotion** (Promoción): a withdrawal to make the business known. Avoid: gift, sample, freebie.
  - **Own use** (Consumo propio): a withdrawal the seller consumes personally. Avoid: personal, shrinkage.
- **Gift** gains the line "Grams shared to promote the business are a promotion, never a gift.", and "promo" leaves its Avoid list.
- **Draw**, **Lot** and **Pending cost** include withdrawn grams.
- **Stock** is everything that came in minus everything sold, gifted or withdrawn.
- **Profit** is sales − cost of goods sold − stock shortfall − promotion cost − own-use cost − expenses.
  - The two new costs are what the withdrawn grams cost, from their lots as they stand today.
  - Pending grams join the "y N g sin costo" count.
  - Caja is unaffected.
- **Days left** and **Stock status** ("no entries") count a withdrawal as grams going out and as movement.
- **Panel**: Semana and Mes gain the two lines.

##### The withdrawal

- **Fields**: one reason (Promoción or Consumo propio), a date, one or more lines of variety and grams, and an optional note.
- **Reason**:
  - set once for the whole withdrawal, never per line;
  - required, with no default: it is chosen every time;
  - a party that mixes both reasons is two withdrawals.
- **Date**: defaults to today in Argentina time. Earlier days are allowed. A future date is refused.
- **Lines**:
  - grams above zero, with at most two decimals;
  - the same variety may appear in several lines, with no merging;
  - a withdrawal with no lines is refused.
- **Variety**: must belong to the business. Any other id answers 404.
- **Note**: optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters. Empty means none. It is never logged, searched or repeated in errors.
- **Refusals**: each carries a code in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) and never repeats the input (ADR-0005).
- **Stock**: recording is never refused for lack of stock (ADR-0009). Stock may go negative and then shows as sold out.
- **Lots and cost**:
  - each line is a draw, taking from the oldest lots of its variety first, in lot order (by date, then recording order);
  - a part beyond every lot is pending cost, never $0;
  - a later stock entry settles the pending part, as for any draw.
- **No money moves**: Caja is untouched.
- **Edit**:
  - every field can change, with the same validation as recording;
  - saving is confirmed first;
  - the lots after it are worked out again.
- **Delete**:
  - a hard delete, confirmed with "Antes de borrar", naming the reason, the date and the total grams;
  - for example "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." or "¿Borrás el consumo propio del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock.";
  - the buttons are "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo";
  - the grams return to stock and land back on lots by date.
- **Never refused because of lots**: neither an edit nor a delete is refused because the withdrawal's grams were already drawn from lots (ADR-0009).

##### Results

- **Inicio**:
  - Semana and Mes show "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" as separate lines, each only when the period has a withdrawal of that reason, like "Faltante de stock";
  - they sit right after Faltante de stock and before Gastos;
  - Hoy shows neither;
  - the fixed "incluye costo pendiente" note appears under each new line when it has pending grams, and under Ganancia when any of its cost is pending.
- **Análisis**:
  - the two lines appear in the chosen month's header, in the week and month rows, and in the since-the-first-record totals;
  - they sit apart from "Faltante de stock";
  - each carries its own "y N g sin costo", counting only its own reason's grams.
- **Ganancia**:
  - it subtracts both lines on each withdrawal's own date;
  - when any of its cost is pending, it is worked out over the costed part, as today;
  - its "y N g sin costo" adds the pending grams sold, gifted, taken by negative adjustments and withdrawn, each counted in the period of its own date.
- **One computation**: every period figure comes from the one period computation Análisis and Inicio already share. There is no second sum.
- **Varieties**: a withdrawal never enters any of these:
  - a variety's table;
  - grams sold or gifted;
  - margin, margin percentage or share of the month;
  - average margin;
  - best week or best weekday.
- **Stock side**: a withdrawal counts
  - as grams going out for days left;
  - as movement for the "no entries" status;
  - as a movement for "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm".

##### Where it is recorded and seen

- **Más (phone)**: "Sacar del stock" sits second, right after "+ Cargar gasto". It opens the form with today's date.
- **A variety's page**: a "Sacar del stock" action sits next to "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio", and opens the form with that variety already chosen. How the three actions sit together is design's call.
- **Variedades (phone and computer)**: a secondary "Sacar del stock" sits next to "+ Entrada de stock". It gives the computer, which has no Más, the same tap budget as the phone.
- **The form**:
  - it is a form under the navigation rule: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", and a confirmation when leaving with something typed;
  - it follows the phone standard for inputs and targets (#80).
- **History**:
  - A withdrawal shows in "Stock y entradas" and its "Ver todas" on the page of each variety it touches.
  - Each row shows the date, "Promoción" or "Consumo propio", and that variety's grams with a minus sign (e.g. "−5 g").
  - Rows mix with stock entries, newest date first, then most recently recorded first.
  - Going out is told by word and sign, never by color.
- **A withdrawal's detail**:
  - It is a detail, with the bar or sidebar and a "‹" back to the variety's page it was opened from. With no history, back goes to Variedades. It shows no "+ Nueva venta".
  - It shows the date, the reason, every line and the note.
  - "Editar" opens the same form, already filled in. "Borrar" asks the confirmation above.
- **No business-wide list.** "Todas las entradas" stays entries only.
- **Navigation docs**:
  - The structure (navigation.md, `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/`, `scripts/design/screens.json`) states the new entries and routes.
  - Routes are in English, and the exact paths are fixed there.
  - A route that shares a first segment with the API follows ADR-0024.

##### Feedback

- **Toasts**: "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no grams or amounts.
- **Refusals** show inline and keep what was typed.
- **A withdrawal that is gone**, following accounts.md:
  - opening it shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades";
  - saving, deleting or confirming on it shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
- **Pinned catalog**: recording, editing and deleting a withdrawal are upkeep triggers, like a stock entry (pinned-catalog.md).

##### Constraints

- Every new table carries `business_id`, and the business comes from the session.
- Every new route joins the isolation sweep, the gated-route test and the ownership migration test.
- Business removal and its export cover the new tables through ADR-0015's schema reflection.
- No note, grams or amount goes to logs, the console or browser storage.
- Copy is in Rioplatense Spanish with vos.

#### Metrics

- **Record time (criterion 1)**:
  - through the front, from a phone viewport, a withdrawal is recorded in under 60 s;
  - the form opens in at most 2 taps from every tab root, on phone and computer viewports;
  - the tap count on a computer is never above the phone's.
- **Stock and lots (criterion 2)**, 100% passing:
  - after a record, an edit and a delete, stock equals in minus out, to the hundredth;
  - editing or deleting a withdrawal works the lots after it out again, and moves only that variety's costs;
  - pending grams are never costed $0;
  - a withdrawal past zero stock is recorded and shows sold out.
- **Ganancia lines (criteria 3, 5)**, 100% passing. The seeded history holds a costed withdrawal of each reason, a withdrawal with pending grams, a negative adjustment and a sale line with pending grams.
  - Inicio's Semana and Mes equal Análisis's row for the same week and month, with 0 differences.
  - Each line lowers Ganancia on its own date.
  - Neither enters Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
  - A period with no withdrawal of a reason shows no line for it.
  - Hoy shows neither line.
  - Each line's pending count counts only its own reason, and Ganancia's count adds every pending gram behind it.
- **Not a sale, not a loss (criterion 4)**: after recording a withdrawal, 0 differences in:
  - sales total and count;
  - average sale;
  - Ventas;
  - every customer's balance and account;
  - Caja;
  - Faltante de stock;
  - every variety's margin and grams sold or gifted.
- **Stock side**:
  - days left counts withdrawn grams;
  - a variety with only a withdrawal is not "sin entradas";
  - "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm" follows the withdrawal's date.
- **Validation matrix**: each of these gives its own coded refusal, which never repeats the input:
  - grams at or below zero;
  - more than two decimals;
  - a future date;
  - a missing reason;
  - no lines;
  - a note over 200 characters;
  - another business's variety.
- **Isolation (criterion 6)**: every withdrawal route is in the sweep and answers 404 or empty from another business, including a reference to another business's variety.
- **No echo**:
  - no refusal body or log line contains the note, grams or amounts;
  - after a full front run, the console and browser storage hold none of them.
- **Front tests**:
  - the form has the form chrome and asks before discarding;
  - the detail has the detail chrome;
  - the confirmations, the toasts and both "Eso ya no está." forms show their fixed text;
  - the history rows are told apart by word and sign;
  - there is no business-wide list.
- **Upkeep**:
  - a withdrawal that changes whether a variety qualifies for the catalog triggers an edit of the pinned message;
  - one that does not change the text sends nothing.
- **No curl**: the end-to-end front run records a withdrawal from Más and from a variety's page, edits and deletes one, and reads Promoción and Consumo propio on Inicio and Análisis.
- **Looks as designed**: before and after comparisons from `scripts/design/compare.sh`, on phone and computer, judged by eye.

#### Out of scope

- Who attended a party, or how many tried.
- Tying a promotion to later sales.
- Money spent on a party. That is an expense.
- A business-wide list of withdrawals.
- A per-variety breakdown of promotion or own-use cost.
- Planned or draft withdrawals.
- Splitting one withdrawal across two reasons.
- Live updates between partners.

#### Dependencies

The screens follow the new navigation (#58/#59) and the phone standard (#80). The backend and glossary can land first.

### For the next role

For the Tech Lead:

- **Cost goes through the existing draw pipeline.** Each withdrawal line is a draw in stock's lot order. Withdrawals reach `allocate` through the same `lots_and_draws` load and the draw-row builder that `SqlDrawCosts` shares (ADR-0010, ADR-0013 amendment).
  - They need a new draw kind beside `sold`, `gifted` and `adjustment`, split by reason.
  - Where a withdrawal sits on an exact tie with a sale line or a negative adjustment is your call. Record it in an ADR if it changes ADR-0010's `LotOrder`.
- **Period figures stay one sum.** Promoción and Consumo propio come from ADR-0020's `scan` and `Buckets`. Inicio and Análisis read the same `PeriodTotals`, and profit's formula is written once.
  - Each new line answers its own pending flag on Inicio and its own pending grams on Análisis.
  - Ganancia's pending count adds the withdrawn grams.
  - Margins, variety rows, grams sold or gifted, best week and best weekday must not see withdrawals.
- **Stock's own figures.** `stock_level` counts withdrawals for stock, status ("no entries", "Sin gramos desde"), days left and pending grams. Total pending still equals `max(0, −stock)`.
- **Mind the module boundary.** Whether withdrawals live in `app/stock/` or a new hexagon is yours. Sales, Ventas, payments and Caja must not read them.
- **Upkeep.** The withdrawal routers trigger upkeep after commit, as stock entries do (ADR-0014). pinned-catalog.md's trigger list is updated.
- **Navigation and routes.**
  - Add the form and the detail to `scripts/design/screens.json`.
  - Add "Sacar del stock" second in Más, on Variedades and on a variety's page.
  - Follow ADR-0024 if the front path shares a segment with the API.
  - New sheets use `useSheet` (ADR-0027). Phone inputs follow #80 (44 px targets, 16 px inputs, decimal keypad for grams).
- **Business removal.**
  - ADR-0015's reflection covers the new tables. The domain may need a declared label for a table without `name`.
  - accounts.md's export list and dry-run kinds will name the new kinds when they are next consolidated. Reflection already exports and deletes them.
- **Tests.** The isolation sweep, gated routes and ownership migration test, plus refusal codes in `contract.json` for every validation-matrix case.

### Clarifications (round 1)

### Answers

**Q1. Is a stock withdrawal one of a business's first records?** Yes. A withdrawal counts as a first record.

The job is that Ganancia shows what promoting the business and my own consumption cost me. A withdrawal dated before every other record would lower Inicio's Ganancia for its week or month and still be missing from Análisis's history and its since-the-first-record totals. That breaks the PRD's criterion 5 (one computation) and Análisis's criterion 2.

What this changes:
- **First record** (glossary) is now the earliest sale, stock entry, stock withdrawal, payment or expense the business recorded in the app.
- **Análisis's history starts there.** A business whose only record is a withdrawal has a history: the withdrawal's week and month show $0 sales and their Promoción or Consumo propio line, and profit goes below zero by that cost.
- **"Nothing to analyse yet"** shows only when the business has no record of any of the five kinds.
- **A new metric in analysis.md**: a withdrawal dated before every other record starts the history. Its cost is in that week's and month's rows and in the since-the-first-record profit. Inicio and Análisis still agree with 0 differences.
- **Nothing else moves.** "Best weekday" still counts sales only. A negative adjustment is already a stock entry, so it is already covered.

This adds to the approved PRD without reopening anything. The PRD's "Results › Análisis" now also reads: "A withdrawal counts as a first record: Análisis's history and its since-the-first-record totals begin at the earliest sale, stock entry, stock withdrawal, payment or expense."

## UX Designer (v1)

### Flow

Source note: the prototype in `docs/product/design-system/pantallas/` has no screen for the withdrawal form ("Sacar del stock") or a withdrawal's detail. Más, Variedades, VariedadDetalle, VerTodas, Inicio, InicioCompu, Analisis and AnalisisCompu exist but show none of the new entries or lines. Each flow below uses the withdrawal's own screens, named by the PRD and navigation.md. Where the current screens cannot show something, it is marked **gap for UX Lead**. I draw no screen.

Touches are taps. Typing a field counts as one touch for the field, and picking an option counts as one. Fields left at their default cost nothing.

### Flow — Record a withdrawal from the phone (job: take grams out without selling them)

**Start**: any tab root on the phone, with today's date and the seller mid-day.

**Path A, from Más (2 taps to the form)**
1. Tab root → tap **Más** (tab bar). Screen: `Mas`. **Gap for UX Lead**: `Mas` does not yet list "Sacar del stock", which the PRD puts second, after "+ Cargar gasto".
2. Tap **Sacar del stock**. The form opens with today's date and no variety chosen.
3. Tap a reason, **Promoción** or **Consumo propio**. This is required and has no default: 1 touch, always.
4. Tap the variety field and pick a variety: 2 touches.
5. Type the grams (decimal keypad): 1 touch. A second variety is "agregar otra línea": 3 more touches per extra line.
6. Optional note and date change, 0 touches by default.
7. Tap **Guardar**.
8. The toast "Retiro guardado" shows, with no grams or amounts. The form closes and goes back by history to the screen that opened it.

Touch count: 2 to open + 1 reason + 2 variety + 1 grams + 1 save = **7 touches** for a one-line withdrawal. Each extra line adds about 3. The form's screen is the withdrawal form, a form-kind screen: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta". No prototype screen exists for it (**gap for UX Lead**).

**Path B, from a variety's page (≤2 taps to the form, variety preset)**
1. Tab root → **Variedades** (1 tap). Screen: `Variedades`, or the two-pane `VariedadesCompu` on a computer.
2. Tap the variety row (1 tap). Screen: `VariedadDetalle`.
3. Tap **Sacar del stock**. This is the third tap, so it is over the ≤2 budget. The budget is met by Path A or C. **Gap for UX Lead**: `VariedadDetalle` shows "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio" but not "Sacar del stock".
4. The form opens with the variety already chosen. Reason (1), grams (1) and save (1) follow.

Touch count: 3 to open + 3 = **6 touches**. If the seller is already on the variety's page: 1 + 3 = **4 touches**. On a computer in the two-pane layout, the variety is selected on the right, so it is 1 tap to open the form.

**Path C, from Variedades (computer and phone)**
1. Tab root → Variedades (1 tap, none if already there). **Gap for UX Lead**: `Variedades` and `VariedadesCompu` show "+ Entrada de stock" but not the secondary "Sacar del stock".
2. Tap **Sacar del stock** (1 tap). The form opens with no variety chosen.
3. Then the same as Path A from step 3.

Touch count: 2 to open + 5 = **7 touches**, the same as Path A. On a computer there is no Más, so Path C is its route. It never takes more taps than the phone.

**Recovery paths**
- **Missing reason**: the save is refused inline on the reason field and everything typed stays. 1 extra touch to choose a reason, then save again (+1).
- **Grams at or below zero, more than two decimals, no lines, future date, note over 200 characters**: each refused inline with its own fixed text, nothing repeated, typed values kept. The seller fixes the field and saves again (+1 to +2).
- **Variety belongs to another business or no longer exists**: the PRD answers 404. This is the "gone" case. The form closes, what was typed is discarded, and the list refreshes with the notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." The seller reopens the form (≥2 more taps). Whether the notice is on the screen the form returns to is a **gap for UX Lead** (`Estados` has the notice).
- **Stock short**: never refused. The withdrawal records, stock goes negative and the variety shows "agotada". The flow does not branch.
- **Seller backs out with something typed**: "Cerrar" or the back gesture asks before discarding, with its own fixed wording. 1 tap to confirm discarding or to keep going (+1). An untouched form closes at once.
- **Sheet over the form** (the variety picker, if it is a sheet): back closes only the top sheet, as its "Cerrar" does (ADR-0027). The phone input standard (#80) applies: 44 px targets, 16 px inputs, decimal keypad.
- **No network**: "Sin conexión". **Server error**: the generic retry. Typed values stay.

**End**: back on the screen that opened the form (Más, Variedades or the variety's page), with "Retiro guardado". The variety's stock, status and days left already reflect the grams, and the new row is first in "Stock y entradas".

**Budget check against the PRD**: ≤2 taps to open from any tab root holds through Más → "Sacar del stock" and Variedades → "Sacar del stock". The variety's page route is 3 taps from a tab root, but it does not carry the budget. Under 60 s: 7 touches is well inside it.

### Flow — See a withdrawal in a variety's history, and open it

**Start**: `VariedadDetalle` of a variety that a withdrawal touched.
1. The section "Stock y entradas" shows the last 3 movements, mixing entries and withdrawals, newest date first. A withdrawal row shows its date, "Promoción" or "Consumo propio", and that variety's grams with a minus sign, for example "−5 g". **Gap for UX Lead**: `VariedadDetalle` and `VerTodas` draw entry rows only, with no row that is told apart by word and sign.
2. For all movements, tap **Ver todas** (1 tap). Screen: `VerTodas`, a card with "‹" back to the variety. It is also the only route to older withdrawals.
3. Tap a withdrawal row (1 tap). Screen: the withdrawal's detail, a card-kind screen with the bar or sidebar, "‹" back to the variety's page it came from, and no "+ Nueva venta". It shows the date, the reason, every line and the note, with "Editar" and "Borrar". No prototype screen exists (**gap for UX Lead**).

Touch count: 1 tap to read the last 3 (the page opens at the variety, so 0 if already there) and 1 tap to open a visible row, so **1 touch**. From a row beyond the first 3: **3 touches** (Ver todas, the row, plus scrolling).

**Recovery paths**
- **Detail opened for a withdrawal that is gone** (deleted by a partner, or another business's id): shows "Eso ya no está." with one button, "Volver a Variedades". 1 tap to return.
- **Back with no history** (a direct link): goes to Variedades.
- **"Todas las entradas"** at the foot of Variedades stays entries only, with no business-wide list. A seller who looks there for a withdrawal does not find it; this is by design and is not a flow to recover.

**End**: on the withdrawal's detail, or back on the variety's page.

### Flow — Edit a withdrawal

**Start**: the withdrawal's detail (see the flow above, 1 to 3 touches to get there).
1. Tap **Editar** (1). The same form opens already filled in.
2. Change any field: the reason, date, lines or note. 1 touch for each field changed, typically 1 or 2.
3. Tap **Guardar** (1). Saving is confirmed first, with its own fixed wording.
4. Tap the confirm button (1).
5. Toast "Retiro guardado". Back to the detail by history.

Touch count: 1 + typically 2 + 1 + 1 = **5 touches** from the detail. From the variety's page: about **6 to 8**.

**Recovery paths**
- Every validation refusal as when recording: inline, values kept.
- **Leave with changes**: asks before discarding (+1).
- **Declined at the confirmation**: the seller stays on the form with everything typed (+1).
- **Withdrawal gone when saving or confirming**: the form closes, the typed values are discarded, and the notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." shows over the refreshed list.
- **A line's grams were already drawn from lots**: never refused. The lots after it are worked out again, and only that variety's costs change.

**End**: the detail shows the new values. The variety's stock, days left and history, and the figures in Inicio and Análisis, follow on their next open.

### Flow — Delete a withdrawal

**Start**: the withdrawal's detail.
1. Tap **Borrar** (1). Screen: the "Antes de borrar" confirmation, for example "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock.", or the same with "el consumo propio", naming the reason, date and total grams.
2. Tap **Sí, borralo** (1). "No, dejalo" cancels.
3. Toast "Retiro eliminado". Back to the variety's page, or Variedades with no history.

Touch count: **2 touches** from the detail. From a variety's page: 3 to 5.

**Recovery paths**
- **"No, dejalo"** (+0 to the end; 1 tap): nothing changes, the seller stays on the detail.
- **Already gone**: confirming shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día.", not the delete's toast. The list refreshes.
- **No network or server error**: "Sin conexión" or the generic retry. The withdrawal stays.

**End**: the variety's page without the row. The grams are back in stock, landing on lots by date.

### Flow — Read Promoción and Consumo propio in Ganancia (job: see what promotion and own use cost me)

**Start**: **Inicio** (tab root, 0 taps if it is the landing screen).

**On Inicio**
1. Tap **Semana** or **Mes** (1 tap). Screen: `Inicio`, or `InicioCompu` in the two-column layout. Hoy shows neither line.
2. The figures read, top to bottom: Costo de lo vendido, Faltante de stock when the period has one, then **Promoción $ X** and **Consumo propio $ X** each only when the period has a withdrawal of that reason, then Gastos, Compras de flores, Ganancia and Caja. **Gap for UX Lead**: the Semana and Mes screens (`Inicio`, `InicioOscuro`, `InicioCompu`) have no Promoción or Consumo propio line, nor the "incluye costo pendiente" note under them.
3. The fixed note "incluye costo pendiente" shows under each new line that has pending grams, and under Ganancia when any of its cost is pending. It carries no grams.

Touch count: **1 touch** (the period tab). 0 more to read.

**On Análisis**
1. Reach Análisis: phone, tab root → **Más** → **Análisis** (2 taps); computer, sidebar → **Análisis** (1 click). Screens: `Analisis`, `AnalisisCompu`.
2. The chosen month's header shows "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" when the month has them, each with its own "y N g sin costo", apart from "Faltante de stock". The week rows (last 8), month rows (last 12) and the since-the-first-record totals show them too. Profit carries "y N g sin costo" adding every pending gram behind it.
3. To read an earlier month, tap the previous-month control (1 tap per month).

Touch count: **2 taps** on the phone to reach it, plus 1 per month moved back. **Gap for UX Lead**: `Analisis` and `AnalisisCompu` show none of the new lines, marks or the withdrawal-free variety table note.

**Recovery paths**
- **A period without a withdrawal of a reason**: no line for it, never a $0 placeholder. This is a stated rule and is not an error.
- **Negative Ganancia**: shown with "−$" in the neutral treatment, as today.
- **Pending grams**: marked by word, "incluye costo pendiente" on Inicio and "y N g sin costo" on Análisis. Never costed $0.
- **No network or server error**: "Sin conexión" or the generic retry.

**End**: the seller has read the two lines on Inicio and Análisis. Both show the same figure for the same week or month.

### Flow — Stock figures after a withdrawal (variety status, days left, Lo que te queda)

This job has no steps of its own. It is the effect the withdrawal flows have on screens the seller already visits.

- **Variedades list and `VariedadDetalle`**: stock grams, the status mark and word, "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm" and days left count the withdrawn grams. A variety with only a withdrawal does not read "Sin entradas". Touches: 0 beyond opening the screen.
- **Inicio → "Lo que te queda"**: the same figures, 0 extra touches.
- **Catalog**: a withdrawal that takes a variety to zero or below changes the catalog text, so the pinned message is edited by upkeep. One that does not change the text sends nothing. No screen is shown, and the seller does no step.

**Recovery**: a failed upkeep leaves the status "Desactualizado" with its fixed reason on the catalog screen and on Inicio's notice. The withdrawal itself still saves.

### Flow — Sales, Ventas, customers and Caja

Unchanged. A withdrawal is not a sale and not a payment, so Nueva venta, Ventas, a sale's detail, Clientes, Registrar cobro and Caja gain no step, no screen and no touch. The flows for those jobs do not change, and no flow is invented for them. The only visible sign is that their figures stay as they were.

### Flow — Record a stock entry, set a threshold, or any other existing job in Variedades

Unchanged in steps and touches. Variedades and a variety's page gain a new action ("Sacar del stock") next to the existing ones. The count of taps to reach "+ Entrada de stock" stays at ≤2, and the placement of the new button is design's call, so it adds no step to the entry flow.

## UX Designer

### Flow

Notes that apply to every job below.
- Touch = one tap, or one field filled (typing counts as one touch per field).
- The prototype (`docs/product/design-system/pantallas`) has no withdrawal form, no withdrawal detail, no withdrawal row and no per-variety movements list. Those flows follow the PRD and the docs (stock.md, navigation.md). I describe them from those docs and draw no screens. Each gap is listed at the end for UX Lead.
- No money moves in a withdrawal. Caja is untouched. A withdrawal is not a sale and is never refused for lack of stock.

### Job: record a withdrawal (grams taken out for Promoción or Consumo propio)

Start: any tab root, usually Inicio or Variedades. There are three entry paths, and all end on the same form.

Path A, phone, from Más
1. Tap "Más" (tab root).
2. On Más, tap "Sacar del stock". It sits second, after "+ Cargar gasto". Form opens (2 taps to open).
3. Choose the reason, Promoción or Consumo propio. It is required and has no default (1 touch).
4. Date shows today. Leave it, or change it. Future dates are not allowed (0 touches by default).
5. Choose the variety (1 touch).
6. Type the grams, with the decimal keypad (1 touch).
7. Optional: type a note, up to 200 characters.
8. Tap Guardar (1 touch).

Total for one line: 7 touches (2 to open, reason, variety, grams, Guardar). Each extra line adds about 2 touches (variety and grams).

Path B, from a variety's page
1. Open the variety from Variedades (tab root, then tap the variety; 2 taps).
2. On the variety's page, tap "Sacar del stock" (3rd tap from a tab root). Form opens with the variety preset.
3. Choose the reason, type the grams, tap Guardar (3 touches).

Total: 6 touches from a tab root. If already on the variety's page: 4 touches (1 to open).

Path C, Variedades (phone and computer)
1. Tap "Variedades" (tab root). On the computer there is no Más, so this is the computer's path.
2. Tap the secondary button "Sacar del stock" (2 taps to open).
3. Reason, variety, grams, Guardar (4 touches).

Total: 6 touches. It is 7 if the date is changed. The count of 7 in Path A comes from the extra step of Más.

End: the form closes and a toast "Retiro guardado" shows. The person lands back where the form was opened. Stock, pending cost and the Ganancia lines update.

What can go wrong and how they recover
- No reason chosen: Guardar is blocked and the reason field is marked. The person chooses one.
- Grams empty, 0 or negative, or more than 2 decimals: the field is marked with a message. The person corrects it.
- Future date: not accepted. The person picks today or earlier.
- Note over 200 characters: the field stops or flags it. The person shortens it.
- More grams than the stock: not an error. The withdrawal saves, and the draw runs in lot order. Pending cost is never $0.
- Leaves the form with unsaved data (Cerrar or back): a confirmation asks before discarding. This is the form chrome: Cerrar, no bar, no "+ Nueva venta".
- A variety deleted meanwhile: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".

Touch count: 6 to 7 for one line, 2 taps to open from any tab root (within the ≤2 budget), except Path B (3 taps).

### Job: see a withdrawal in a variety's history and open it

Start: the variety's page, section "Stock y entradas".
1. The section shows the last 3 movements, entries and withdrawals together. A withdrawal row shows the date, the reason word and "−5 g".
2. Tap the withdrawal row. Its detail opens as a card (1 touch). The card has "‹" back to the variety's page, no history entry and no "+ Nueva venta". It has Editar and Borrar.

Older withdrawals (not among the last 3)
1. Tap "Ver todas" in "Stock y entradas" (1 touch).
2. The docs require a per-variety movements list there. It holds that variety's entries and withdrawals, newest date first, then most recently recorded. It is a card with "‹" back to the variety's page.
3. Tap the row (1 touch). Total 2 touches plus scrolling.

This list is not `TodasLasEntradas`, which stays business-wide and entries only, with no business-wide list of withdrawals. It is also not `VerTodas`, which is this variety's Precios list. In the prototype, "Ver todas" in "Stock y entradas" opens `TodasLasEntradas`, so the prototype cannot support this route. This is a gap, not drawn here.

What can go wrong and how they recover
- The withdrawal was deleted meanwhile (for example on another device): the card shows "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
- The list is stale: the notice "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." shows and the list refreshes.

End: on the withdrawal's detail card. Back goes to the variety's page.

Touch count: 1 for the last 3 movements, 2 for older ones.

### Job: edit a withdrawal

Start: the withdrawal's detail card (see above).
1. Tap Editar (1 touch). The form opens with the saved values.
2. Change reason, date, a line, grams or note (typically 2 touches).
3. Tap Guardar (1 touch). A confirmation is asked before saving (1 touch to confirm).

End: back on the detail, toast "Retiro guardado". Stock, pending cost and the Ganancia lines are recalculated.

Touch count: about 5 from the detail, 6 from the variety's page, plus the touches of the changes. Edit is allowed at any time.

Recovery
- Same field errors as when recording.
- Leaves with unsaved changes: a confirmation asks before discarding.
- Declines the save confirmation: stays on the form with the changes.
- The withdrawal is gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".

### Job: delete a withdrawal

Start: the withdrawal's detail card.
1. Tap Borrar (1 touch). The sheet "Antes de borrar" opens, with history-backed back (ADR-0027).
2. Tap "Sí, borralo" (1 touch). "No, dejalo" closes the sheet and changes nothing.

End: toast "Retiro eliminado". The person returns to the variety's page. The grams go back to the lots, and the lines in Ganancia are recalculated.

Touch count: 2 from the detail, 3 from the variety's page. Delete is allowed at any time.

Recovery: "No, dejalo" or back closes the sheet. If the withdrawal is already gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".

### Job: read Promoción and Consumo propio in Ganancia (Inicio)

Start: Inicio.
1. On the Semana or Mes tab, "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" show after Faltante de stock and before Gastos, only when the period has them. Under each line, and under Ganancia, the fixed note "incluye costo pendiente" shows.
2. Hoy shows neither line.

Touch count: 1 tap on the Semana or Mes tab, 0 if Inicio already shows it. Nothing else changes in how Inicio is read. The new lines are not drawn in `Inicio`, `InicioCompu` or `InicioOscuro`. This is a gap.

Recovery: if the period has no withdrawals, the lines are simply absent. This is not an error.

### Job: read Promoción and Consumo propio in Análisis

Start: Análisis.
1. On the phone: Más, then Análisis (2 taps). On the computer: the Análisis tab (1 tap).
2. The month header, the week and month rows, and the since-the-first-record totals show the two reasons, with "y N g sin costo" per reason when it applies.
3. To go to an earlier month, tap once per month moved back.

A withdrawal never enters a variety's table, margin, grams, best week or best weekday. Those figures are unchanged.

Touch count: 2 on the phone, 1 on the computer, plus 1 per month moved back. The new lines are not drawn in `Analisis` or `AnalisisCompu`. This is a gap.

### Job: check stock after a withdrawal

Start: Variedades or a variety's page.
1. The variety's stock figure is lower by the grams withdrawn, and pending cost follows the draw in lot order (ADR-0009, ADR-0010).
2. Reading it takes 0 touches after saving, because the person lands where the form opened. On the variety's page it takes 1 tap from Variedades.

Unchanged flows: sales and Ventas, customers, Caja, stock entries (EntradaStock) and "Todas las entradas" are unchanged by this task. Their steps, screens and touch counts stay as they were. Upkeep changes only when the pinned catalog text changes; a withdrawal alone does not trigger an edit there.

### Gaps for UX Lead (screens the current prototype does not draw)
- The withdrawal form (phone and computer), with its entries "Sacar del stock" in Más, in `VariedadDetalle` and in `Variedades`/`VariedadesCompu`.
- The withdrawal's detail card with Editar and Borrar, and its "Antes de borrar" sheet.
- The withdrawal row inside "Stock y entradas".
- The per-variety movements list opened by "Stock y entradas" → "Ver todas". The prototype sends that link to `TodasLasEntradas`.
- "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" lines on `Inicio`, `InicioCompu`, `InicioOscuro`, `Analisis` and `AnalisisCompu`.
- Path B takes 3 taps from a tab root, one over the ≤2 budget, because the variety's page sits behind Variedades.

## UX Lead (v1)

_The system denied 2 write attempts: this role writes nothing; reproduce by reading the code, the task documents and the existing tests, or by running the declared checks. A denial here is not a syntax problem._

### Walk

**Record a withdrawal (phone and computer)**
- *Path A, `Mas`*: the prototype lists + Cargar gasto, Gastos, Análisis and Configuración. "Sacar del stock" is not there, as the flow says. Putting it second keeps the 2-tap budget.
- *Form*: **sin prototipo**. I judged it on the text only. The touch count (7 touches for one line) holds and fits inside 60 s. The reason is required with no default, and refusals keep what was typed.
- *Path B, `VariedadDetalle`*: two 52 px pills in a row, "Entrada" and "Nuevo precio". "Sacar del stock" is missing, and a third pill at 390 px is tight (observation).
- *Path C, `Variedades` and `VariedadesCompu`*: only "Entrada de stock" (+ "Entrada" and "Nuevo precio" in the computer's right pane). The secondary button is missing. The computer's budget holds on the text.
- *Recovery when a variety is gone*: discarding a whole multi-line withdrawal is costly, but it follows accounts.md (observation).

**See a withdrawal in a variety's history and open it**
- *`VariedadDetalle` → "Stock y entradas"*: the prototype shows one entry row ("26/08 · Compra", 40 g, $ 280.000). There is no withdrawal row design.
- *"Ver todas" (blocking)*: in the prototype this link goes to **`TodasLasEntradas`**, not to `VerTodas`. `TodasLasEntradas` is the business-wide entries list, with "‹ Variedades" and per-row Editar/Borrar. `VerTodas` is the variety's **Precios** list, reached from the "Precios" section's own "Ver todas".
  - The flow calls `VerTodas` "the only route to older withdrawals".
  - But the screen that link actually opens must stay entries only, by the PRD.
  - So a withdrawal older than the rows shown on the page has no route to it. It cannot be opened, edited or deleted. This is a dead end.
- *Withdrawal detail*: **sin prototipo**. On the text, the chrome, the back target and Editar/Borrar are consistent.

**Edit and delete a withdrawal**
- Both start on the detail, which is **sin prototipo**. On the text, the steps, confirmations, toasts and "Eso ya no está." recoveries are sound.
- Both inherit the dead end above: only recent withdrawals are reachable.

**Read Promoción and Consumo propio**
- *`Inicio` and `InicioCompu`*: the Semana/Mes figures show Costo de lo vendido, Gastos, Ganancia and Caja. No "Faltante de stock" is drawn, so the placement "after Faltante" can only be checked against text. The new lines and the "incluye costo pendiente" note are missing, as the flow says.
- *`Analisis` and `AnalisisCompu`*: reached through Más → Análisis in 2 taps, as stated. None of the new lines or their "y N g sin costo" are there.

**Stock effects; sales, customers and Caja; other Variedades jobs**: no steps of their own. Nothing in the prototype contradicts the flow.

### Verdict

**returned**: UX Designer must name a per-variety movements list that "Stock y entradas" → "Ver todas" opens, with entries and withdrawals and a back to the variety. In the prototype that link opens the business-wide `TodasLasEntradas`, which must stay entries only, so older withdrawals are unreachable.

## UI Designer (v1)

### Screens

General rules for every screen below: composed only from the "Papel y azul" tokens and the existing prototype screens. Paper background, 24px side padding, Plus Jakarta Sans, 52px fields on a `#F2EEE6` fill with 16px radius, 14px labels in `--tenue` ink, 44px pills, a 56px dark pill as the main action. No state is shown by colour alone. The withdrawal reason is always a word, and the quantity always carries a "−" sign. Blue and amber are the only state hues, never green or red. Phone is 390px and computer reuses the same pieces in the Compu layouts. Dark mode comes from the tokens, with nothing drawn per screen.

### Screens — Sacar del stock form (new screen; phone and computer)

- **Built from:** `EntradaStock` and `EntradaStockCompu` (form chrome: "Nueva entrada" header with "Cerrar" on the right, no bottom bar, 28px-radius sheet, footer with a result figure and a 56px dark pill).
  - Title: "Sacar del stock". Reached from Más, Variedades and the variedad detail. It opens as a sheet through `useSheet`, so the back gesture works like "Cerrar".
- **Fields, in order:**
  - Fecha. Same date field as EntradaStock, defaulting to today.
  - Gramos. Same 120px field as EntradaStock, `inputmode="decimal"`.
  - Variedad. Same 60px picker row as EntradaStock, with the "Hoy tiene N g" subline.
  - Motivo. Same pill group as "Origen" in EntradaStock, with two pills: "Promoción" and "Consumo propio". Nothing is selected at first, following the required-choice pattern of `GastoNuevo`. The selected pill is dark-filled and also marked with a check icon, so it isn't colour alone.
  - Nota. Same optional 52px field.
- **Multiple lines:** one withdrawal can hold several varieties. Use the "line" pattern from the sales form (`NuevaVenta`): each line holds Variedad and Gramos, with an "+ Agregar otra variedad" text action below the lines and a remove icon with a 44px target per line. Motivo, Fecha and Nota apply to the whole withdrawal.
- **Footer:** the left side reads "Costo que sale del stock", and the right side shows the figure in 30px weight 600, with "−N g" beside it. The pill reads "Guardar retiro". Cost is the one calculated by drawing in lot order. The footer does not show a sale price.
- **States:**
  - Initial and empty: Motivo has no choice, the pill is disabled-looking but still focusable, and tapping it shows the refusals.
  - Loading (varieties and stock figures): the shared skeleton from `Estados`, with the fields drawn but no figures.
  - Filled: all fields set and the footer figure shown.
  - Field refusal (inline, 13px text under the field, with the typed text kept): "Elegí un motivo", "Poné los gramos", "Elegí la variedad", "La fecha no es válida".
  - Over stock: the withdrawal is allowed. Under Variedad, a 13px note says "Quedaría en −N g", in words and with the sign, not red. Nothing blocks saving.
  - Pending cost: when a lot has no cost, the footer figure is replaced by "Costo pendiente", never "$ 0". The 13px line says "Se completa cuando cargues el costo".
  - Saving: the pill shows the busy state used in the other forms, and it is not tappable twice.
  - Offline and server error: the shared `Estados` banner above the footer, with the form kept as typed and "Reintentar".
  - Discard confirmation: closing with typed data shows the same "¿Descartar?" confirmation as the other forms.
  - Success: the sheet closes and the toast "Retiro guardado" appears.
- **Edit:** the same form opens titled "Editar retiro", with the values filled in and a single line, since a stored withdrawal is one variety. The pill reads "Guardar cambios". Before saving, it shows the confirmation that other edits already show.
- **PRD change:** this is the new capture screen. It adds no field to the existing entry or sales forms.

### Screens — Withdrawal detail (new screen; phone and computer)

- **Built from:** `VentaDetalle` and `VentaDetalleCompu` (detail chrome: "‹ Variedades" or "‹ parent", with the bar or sidebar and no "+ Nueva venta"; fields as label and value rows; "Editar" and "Borrar" actions).
- **Content, top to bottom:**
  - A heading with "−N g" in 30px weight 600 and the variety name.
  - Rows for Fecha, Motivo (word), Costo, and Nota.
  - Costo shows the amount that left the stock, or "Costo pendiente" in amber with a clock icon and the word, when pending.
- **Delete:** "Borrar" opens the "Antes de borrar" confirmation used elsewhere. It says the grams go back to the stock and the figures update. The buttons are "Borrar retiro" and "Cancelar". Success shows the toast "Retiro eliminado" and returns to the list the user came from.
- **States:** loading (shared skeleton), filled, pending cost, gone ("Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades", as in the other details), offline, and error (from `Estados`).
- **PRD change:** the screen is new. It reuses the existing detail structure.

### Screens — Más (phone) and its computer equivalent

- **Built from:** `Mas`. Add one row, "Sacar del stock", directly after "+ Cargar gasto", using the same row style, with a leading icon from the existing icon set and a chevron. Nothing else on the screen changes.
- **States:** the screen has no loading or empty state of its own, so it adds none.
- **PRD change:** one new entry point.

### Screens — Variedades and VariedadesCompu

- **Built from:** `Variedades` and `VariedadesCompu`. Next to the primary "+ Entrada de stock" pill, add a secondary action, "Sacar del stock", using the 44px grey `#F2EEE6` pill style seen in the secondary controls. On the phone the two pills sit in one row, each with a 44px target. If they do not fit at 390px, "Sacar del stock" wraps to a second row, with no smaller text.
- **States:** the list's loading, empty, filled and error states are unchanged. In the empty state, "Sacar del stock" is still shown but its screen then shows "Hoy tiene 0 g" for every variety.
- **PRD change:** one new action, with no change to the rows.

### Screens — VariedadDetalle (phone and computer)

- **Built from:** `VariedadDetalle`.
- **Actions:** add a third action, "Sacar del stock", with the variety preselected. The existing two pills are 52px, and a third does not fit on one row at 390px. See gap 2.
- **History ("Stock y entradas"):** withdrawals appear in the same list as entries, ordered by date. A withdrawal row uses the entry-row layout: date and motive word on the left ("Promoción" or "Consumo propio"), "−N g" on the right in weight 600. Entries keep their "+N g". The sign and the word carry the distinction, not colour. A pending-cost withdrawal shows "Costo pendiente" as the row's second line, with the amber clock icon.
- **Tapping a row:** a withdrawal row opens the withdrawal detail, and an entry row opens the entry as it does now.
- **States:** loading and gone ("Eso ya no está.") as today. With no movements, the existing empty state is kept.
- **PRD change:** the history now includes withdrawals, and the stock figure at the top already reflects them.

### Screens — Variety movements list ("Ver todas")

- **Built from:** `TodasLasEntradas` and `VerTodas`, with the same row layout as the history above.
- **Scope:** for one variety, it lists entries and withdrawals together. See gap 3 for the business-wide list, which stays entries-only.
- **States:** loading, empty, filled, and error from `Estados`. After a delete, the toast "Retiro eliminado" appears and the list updates, with "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." if the row had already gone.

### Screens — Inicio, InicioCompu, InicioOscuro

- **Built from:** the Ganancia block of `Inicio` on its Semana and Mes tabs. Add two lines, "Promoción" and "Consumo propio", after "Faltante de stock" and before "Gastos". Each uses the existing line style, with a label on the left and an amount on the right, shown as a deduction with the "−" sign.
- **Hoy tab:** no change. It shows no withdrawal lines.
- **Pending cost:** only when a line has pending cost, the note "incluye costo pendiente" appears under the line, fixed text with no grams, in the same note style as the other lines with pending cost. The amount is never shown as $ 0.
- **States:** loading shows the shared skeleton, with no figures. A period without withdrawals shows the two lines at "$ 0", with no note, or hides them if the existing screen hides empty lines. See gap 4. Offline and error come from `Estados`.
- **First-time screens (`InicioPrimeraVez`, Compu):** no change, since nothing has been withdrawn.
- **Dark:** `InicioOscuro` takes the same lines through the tokens.
- **PRD change:** two new Ganancia lines. Their totals are the same computation as the rest of the period figures, so Ganancia on this screen and in Análisis agree.

### Screens — Analisis and AnalisisCompu

- **Built from:** `Analisis` and `AnalisisCompu`. Add "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" rows in the month header, in the week and month rows, and in the totals since the first record, using the same row pattern as the existing cost lines.
- **Pending cost:** each reason shows "y N g sin costo" as a subline when part of its grams has no cost, in the style of the existing pending notes. Unlike Inicio, this note carries the grams.
- **States:** loading, filled, no data for the period (the existing empty state), offline and error from `Estados`.
- **PRD change:** the new rows and sublines. The figures come from the one computation Inicio also uses.

### Screens — Estados and toasts

- **Built from:** `Estados`, used unchanged.
  - Loading: the shared skeleton.
  - Offline: the shared banner.
  - Error: the shared panel with "Reintentar".
  - Gone: "Eso ya no está." in a detail, with "Volver a Variedades"; "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." in a list.
- **Toasts:** "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", in the existing toast style. A toast announces, and it carries no action.
- **PRD change:** two new toast texts only.

### Screens — Gaps for UI Lead

1. **Multiple-line form:** the prototype's line pattern lives in the sales form (`NuevaVenta`). The sales form's visual-viewport pinning (ADR-0028) is not planned for this form. Confirm that the line pattern is reusable here without the pinning.
2. **VariedadDetalle action row:** there is no drawn layout for three actions at 390px. Proposed: the third action wraps to a second row, or "Sacar del stock" becomes a secondary pill. UI Lead decides, with no new component.
3. **"Ver todas" scope:** the prototype routes "Ver todas" to the business-wide `TodasLasEntradas`, which must stay entries-only. The per-variety movements list (entries plus withdrawals) is not drawn in the prototype. It is built here from the same rows.
4. **Inicio and Faltante de stock:** "Faltante de stock" is itself not drawn in `Inicio`. The placement "after Faltante de stock, before Gastos" has no reference line to copy. The empty-line rule (show at "$ 0" or hide) is also undrawn.
5. **Pending-cost marker:** the amber clock icon and word are borrowed from the existing pending notes. If there is no equivalent drawn on a list row, UI Lead should confirm it.
6. **Withdrawal detail and delete confirmation:** neither is drawn for withdrawals. They are assembled from `VentaDetalle` and the existing "Antes de borrar" confirmation.

## UI Designer

### Screens

Rules for every screen below: composed only from the "Papel y azul" tokens and the product's existing screens and components. The prototype draws no withdrawal form, detail, history row, per-variety movements list or Promoción/Consumo propio line. Each is assembled from existing pieces, and each such place is named. Nothing new is drawn outside what exists. Every state is told by word and shape, never by colour alone (blue ↔ amber, never green ↔ red). The reason is always a word, and grams going out always carry a "−" sign. Dark comes from the tokens, with nothing drawn per screen.

### Screens — Sacar del stock form (new; phone and computer)

- **Built from:** `EntradaStock` / `EntradaStockCompu` for the form chrome (title, "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", 56 px dark pill footer). The line pattern comes from `NuevaVenta`'s lines (variety + grams row, "+ Agregar otra variedad", remove control of at least 44 px). Opened as a sheet through `useSheet` (ADR-0027), so the back gesture does what "Cerrar" does.
- **Title:** "Sacar del stock". Pill: "Guardar retiro".
- **Field order, top to bottom:**
  1. Motivo: the pill group of EntradaStock's "Origen", with two pills, "Promoción" and "Consumo propio". Nothing is chosen at first (required, no default, as GastoNuevo's category). The chosen pill is dark-filled and also carries a check, so it is not colour alone.
  2. Fecha: today by default, shown like NuevaVenta's date, never a later day.
  3. Lines, one or more: Variedad (the 60 px picker row with "Hoy tiene N g") and Gramos (`inputmode="decimal"`, at least 16 px, `enterkeyhint`). The same variety may repeat, with no merging. "+ Agregar otra variedad" adds a line. A line can be removed while more than one remains.
  4. Nota: optional 52 px field.
- **Footer:** "Costo que sale del stock" with the figure, and the total "−N g" on one compact line. When any part is pending the figure reads "Costo pendiente", never "$ 0", with the line "Se completa cuando cargues el costo". The footer carries no sale price. Because there is no sale total pinned to the keyboard, the sheet scrolls normally. The footer stays visible with the keyboard open, checked at 390 px (gap 1).
- **States:**
  - Loading (varieties and stock figures): the shared loading state of `Estados`, fields drawn and no figure or digit.
  - Empty and untouched: no reason chosen, one empty line. Guardar shows the refusals when tapped.
  - Filled: all fields set and the footer figure shown.
  - Inline refusals, fixed text that keeps what was typed and never repeats the input: "Elegí un motivo", "Elegí la variedad", "Poné los gramos", "Los gramos tienen hasta dos decimales", "La fecha no puede ser futura", "La nota tiene hasta 200 caracteres", "Agregá al menos una variedad".
  - Over stock: allowed. Under the variety a 13 px note in words and sign, "Quedaría en −N g", in neutral ink. Nothing blocks saving.
  - Saving: the pill shows the busy state of the other forms, not tappable twice.
  - Offline ("Sin conexión") and server error (generic retry): the shared `Estados` states, with the form kept as typed.
  - Discard: closing or going back with something typed asks "¿Descartás el retiro?" with "No, seguí cargando / Sí, descartalo", in the card of Nueva venta's discard question. An untouched form closes at once.
  - A variety gone while open: saving closes the form and shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
  - Success: toast "Retiro guardado".
- **Edit:** the same form, titled "Editar retiro", opened from the detail's "Editar" with every saved field filled in: reason, date, **all saved lines** (with add and remove, like a new withdrawal) and note. The pill reads "Guardar cambios". Saving is confirmed first with the product's confirm card, and "No" returns to the form with the changes kept. Once saved, the toast is "Retiro guardado".
- **PRD change:** this is the new capture screen. It changes no existing form.

## PM: consolidation

### Verdict

**approved**: UX Lead and UI Lead both approved, and neither marked anything as blocking. Every job can be completed, so the remaining friction goes to the pull request as observations.

### PRD

#### Problem

Grams leave a business's stock without anyone buying them. The seller takes a few grams to a party so several people can try them, or consumes some personally. Today the app offers only two ways to record that, and both distort the books:

- **A sale without a customer made of gift lines.** It counts a sale nobody made, and inflates the sales count, the average sale and Ventas.
- **A negative adjustment.** It reads as missing flower and lands in Faltante de stock.

Some grams are never recorded at all. Stock then drifts until the next count corrects it as shortfall. Ganancia has no line for what promotion and own use cost.

#### Job to be done

**When** I take grams out of my own stock without selling them (to share at a party so people get to know the business, or for my own use), **I want** to record them from my phone in a few taps (date, varieties and grams, the reason), **so I can** keep stock right and see in Ganancia what promoting the business and my own consumption cost me, without recording a sale nobody made or an adjustment that reads as lost flower.

This refines BRIEF.md's stock job and its results job; it is not a new top-level job.

- A withdrawal records grams that have already left stock, dated the day they left.
- A seller catching up later sets an earlier date.
- There are no planned or draft withdrawals.

#### Success criteria

1. **Fast from the phone.** A withdrawal is recorded in under a minute. The form opens in at most 2 taps from any tab root. On a computer it takes no more taps than on the phone.
2. **Stock stays right.** Once a withdrawal is recorded, edited or deleted, the change shows at once in the variety's stock, status, days left and history, and in the lots after it. Pending grams are pending, never $0.
3. **Its own lines in Ganancia.** Inicio's Semana and Mes, and Análisis, show "Promoción" and "Consumo propio" as separate lines at lot cost.
   - Each lowers Ganancia on its own date.
   - Pending cost is marked the way it is today.
   - Neither is ever folded into Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
4. **Not a sale, not a loss.** These stay exactly as they were: sales count, sales total, average sale, Ventas, every customer's history and Caja. Faltante de stock never includes a withdrawal.
5. **One computation.** Semana's and Mes's Promoción, Consumo propio and Ganancia equal Análisis's figures for the same week and month, with 0 differences (ADR-0020, ADR-0013 amendment).
6. **Businesses never mix** (ADR-0004).

#### Behaviour

##### Glossary (CONTEXT.md)

- **Stock withdrawal**: in the app it is a Retiro, recorded with the action "Sacar del stock".
- **New terms:**
  - **Promotion** (Promoción): a withdrawal to make the business known.
  - **Own use** (Consumo propio): a withdrawal the seller consumes personally.
- **Gift** gains the line "Grams shared to promote the business are a promotion, never a gift.", and "promo" leaves its Avoid list.
- **Draw, Lot and Pending cost** include withdrawn grams.
- **Stock** is everything that came in minus everything sold, gifted or withdrawn.
- **Profit** is sales − cost of goods sold − stock shortfall − promotion cost − own-use cost − expenses.
  - The two new costs are what the withdrawn grams cost, from their lots as they stand today.
  - Pending grams join the "y N g sin costo" count.
  - Caja is unaffected.
- **Days left** and **Stock status** ("no entries") count a withdrawal as grams going out and as movement.
- **Panel**: Semana and Mes gain the two lines.

##### The withdrawal

- **Fields**: one reason (Promoción or Consumo propio), a date, one or more lines of variety and grams, and an optional note.
- **Reason**: set once for the whole withdrawal, never per line. It is required, with no default. A party that mixes both reasons is two withdrawals.
- **Date**: defaults to today in Argentina time. Earlier days are allowed; a future date is refused.
- **Lines**: grams above zero, with at most two decimals. The same variety may appear in several lines, with no merging. A withdrawal with no lines is refused.
- **Variety**: must belong to the business. Any other id answers 404.
- **Note**: optional, trimmed, up to 200 characters. Empty means none. It is never logged, searched or repeated in errors.
- **Refusals**: each carries a code in `contract.json` (ADR-0008) and never repeats the input (ADR-0005).
- **Stock**:
  - Recording is never refused for lack of stock (ADR-0009). Stock may go negative and then shows as sold out.
  - Each line is a draw in lot order: by date, then recording order.
  - A part beyond every lot is pending cost, never $0. A later stock entry settles it.
- **No money moves**: Caja is untouched.
- **Edit**: every field can change, with the same validation as recording. Saving is confirmed first, and the lots after it are worked out again.
- **Delete**:
  - It is a hard delete, confirmed with "Antes de borrar", naming the reason, date and total grams. The grams land back on lots by date.
  - Neither an edit nor a delete is refused because the withdrawal's grams were already drawn from lots.

##### Results

- **Inicio**:
  - Semana and Mes show "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" as separate lines. They sit right after Faltante de stock and before Gastos, and each shows only when the period has a withdrawal of that reason.
  - Hoy shows neither line.
  - The fixed "incluye costo pendiente" shows under a line only when that line has pending grams, and under Ganancia when any of its cost is pending. It never shows under Caja.
- **Análisis**:
  - The two lines appear in the chosen month's header (apart from Faltante de stock), in the week and month rows, and in the since-the-first-record totals.
  - Each line carries its own "y N g sin costo", counting only its own reason's grams.
- **Ganancia**:
  - It subtracts both lines on each withdrawal's own date. With pending cost, it is worked out over the costed part.
  - Its "y N g sin costo" adds the pending grams sold, gifted, negatively adjusted and withdrawn, each in the period of its own date.
- **Varieties**: a withdrawal never enters a variety's table, grams sold or gifted, margin, margin percentage, share of the month, average margin, best week or best weekday.
- **Stock side**: a withdrawal counts for days left, for the "no entries" status and for "Sin gramos desde el dd/mm".

##### Constraints

- New tables carry `business_id`, and the business comes from the session.
- New routes join the isolation sweep, the gated-route test and the ownership migration test.
- Business removal covers the new tables through ADR-0015's schema reflection.
- No note, grams or amount goes to logs, the console or browser storage.
- Recording, editing and deleting a withdrawal are upkeep triggers for the pinned catalog. The pinned message is edited only when the catalog text changes.
- Copy is in Rioplatense Spanish with vos.

#### Flows

A touch is one tap, or one field filled.

##### Job: record a withdrawal

The flow starts at any tab root. All three paths end on the same "Sacar del stock" form.

- **Path A (phone, Más)**:
  1. Más.
  2. "Sacar del stock", second after "+ Cargar gasto". The form opens with today's date and no variety chosen (2 taps).
  3. Reason.
  4. Variety.
  5. Grams.
  6. Optional note.
  7. "Guardar retiro".

  One line takes **6 touches**. Each extra line adds about 2.
- **Path B (a variety's page)**:
  1. Variedades.
  2. The variety.
  3. "Sacar del stock". The form opens with the variety chosen (3 taps from a tab root).
  4. Reason.
  5. Grams.
  6. Guardar.

  That is 6 touches, or 4 if the seller is already on the page.
- **Path C (Variedades, phone and computer; the computer's path)**:
  1. Variedades.
  2. The secondary "Sacar del stock" (2 taps).
  3. Reason.
  4. Variety.
  5. Grams.
  6. Guardar.

  That is **6 touches**.
- **End**: the form closes with the toast "Retiro guardado", and the seller lands where the form was opened. Stock, pending cost and the Ganancia lines update.
- **Recovery**:
  - A missing reason, a missing variety, missing or invalid grams, a future date, a note that is too long or no lines: Guardar shows the inline refusal and keeps what was typed.
  - More grams than in stock is not an error. The form notes "Quedaría en −N g" and saves.
  - Leaving with something typed asks "¿Descartás el retiro?".
  - A variety that is gone when saving closes the form and shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

##### Job: see a withdrawal in a variety's history and open it

1. On the variety's page, "Stock y entradas" shows the last 3 movements, entries and withdrawals together.
2. A withdrawal row shows the date, the reason word and "−5 g".
3. Tapping the row opens the withdrawal's detail (1 touch).

Older movements:

1. "Ver todas" opens the variety's own movements list: entries and withdrawals, newest date first, then most recently recorded first.
2. Tap a row (2 touches plus scrolling).

The movements list is not "Todas las entradas", which stays business-wide and entries only.

- **End**: the seller is on the withdrawal's detail. Back goes to the variety's page, or to Variedades with no history.
- **Recovery**:
  - A withdrawal that is gone opens as "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades".
  - A stale list shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día." and refreshes.

##### Job: edit a withdrawal

1. On the detail, "Editar" opens "Editar retiro" with every saved field and every line filled in.
2. Change what is needed.
3. "Guardar cambios".
4. Confirm.

That is about 4 touches plus the changes.

- **End**: back on the detail, with the toast "Retiro guardado". Lots, stock and Ganancia are worked out again.
- **Recovery**:
  - Field errors are the same as when recording.
  - Declining the confirmation keeps the changes on the form.
  - Leaving with changes asks before discarding.
  - A withdrawal that is gone shows "Eso ya no está."

##### Job: delete a withdrawal

1. On the detail, tap "Borrar".
2. "Antes de borrar" asks "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." (or "el consumo propio").
3. Tap "Sí, borralo".

That is 2 touches.

- **End**: the toast "Retiro eliminado", then back to the variety's page (Variedades with no history). The grams return to their lots.
- **Recovery**:
  - "No, dejalo" or back closes the card and changes nothing.
  - A withdrawal already gone shows "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."

##### Job: read Promoción and Consumo propio

- **Inicio**: on the Semana or Mes tab (0 or 1 tap), the lines show after Faltante de stock and before Gastos, only when the period has them.
- **Análisis**: Más → Análisis on the phone (2 taps), or the sidebar on a computer (1 tap), plus 1 tap per month moved back.
- A period with no withdrawals shows no line. That is not an error.

##### Job: check stock after a withdrawal

The variety's stock, status, days left and history already reflect the withdrawal on Variedades and on the variety's page. Reading them takes 0 or 1 tap after saving.

Sales, Ventas, customers, Caja, stock entries and "Todas las entradas" keep their flows unchanged.

#### Screens

The screens use only the "Papel y azul" tokens and the product's existing components. Every state is told by word and shape, never by color alone. The reason is always a word, and grams going out always carry "−". Dark comes from the tokens.

##### Sacar del stock form (new, phone and computer)

- **Built from**:
  - `EntradaStock` / `EntradaStockCompu`: "Cerrar", no bar, no "+ Nueva venta", and the 56 px dark pill.
  - `NuevaVenta`: the line pattern.
  - `useSheet` (ADR-0027): back does what "Cerrar" does.
- **Title** "Sacar del stock"; **pill** "Guardar retiro".
- **Fields**, in this order:
  1. **Motivo**: the "Origen" pill group, with "Promoción" and "Consumo propio". Nothing is chosen at first. The chosen pill is filled and carries a check.
  2. **Fecha**: today by default, never later.
  3. **Lines**: the variety picker row with "Hoy tiene N g", and grams (`inputmode="decimal"`, at least 16 px, `enterkeyhint`).
     - "+ Agregar otra variedad" adds a line.
     - A line can be removed while more than one remains.
  4. **Nota**: optional.
- **Footer**:
  - "Costo que sale del stock" with its figure. When any part is pending it reads "Costo pendiente", never "$ 0", with "Se completa cuando cargues el costo".
  - The total "−N g" on the same compact line.
- **States**:
  - Loading: the shared state, with no digit.
  - Untouched: no reason chosen and one empty line.
  - Filled.
  - Inline refusals:
    - "Elegí un motivo"
    - "Elegí la variedad"
    - "Poné los gramos"
    - "Los gramos tienen hasta dos decimales"
    - "La fecha no puede ser futura"
    - "La nota tiene hasta 200 caracteres"
    - "Agregá al menos una variedad"
  - Over stock: "Quedaría en −N g", in neutral ink.
  - Saving: busy pill.
  - Errors: "Sin conexión" or the generic retry, with the form kept as typed.
  - Discard: "¿Descartás el retiro?", with "No, seguí cargando / Sí, descartalo".
  - Variety gone on save: "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
  - Success: the toast "Retiro guardado".
- **Edit**: titled "Editar retiro", with every saved field and line filled in and add and remove kept. The pill reads "Guardar cambios", and saving is confirmed first.

##### Withdrawal detail (new, phone and computer)

- **Built from**: `VentaDetalle` / `VentaDetalleCompu`: the bar or sidebar, "‹" to the variety's page it came from (Variedades with no history), and no "+ Nueva venta".
- **Content**:
  - Heading: the reason word and the total "−N g".
  - Fecha.
  - Nota, left out when there is none.
  - One row per saved line, never merged: variety and "−N g".
  - Costo: the amount, or "Costo pendiente" with the clock icon.
- **Actions**:
  - "Editar".
  - "Borrar", which opens Estados' "Antes de borrar" card: "¿Borrás la promoción del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock." / "¿Borrás el consumo propio del 03/10? Vuelven 5 g al stock.", with "No, dejalo / Sí, borralo". After deleting: the toast "Retiro eliminado", then back.
- **States**:
  - Loading.
  - Filled.
  - Pending.
  - Gone: "Eso ya no está." with "Volver a Variedades", and no id, name or date.
  - Gone on confirm: "Eso ya no está. La lista ya está al día."
  - Offline and error.

##### Más (phone)

One row, "Sacar del stock", after "+ Cargar gasto" and before Gastos, in the same row style with an existing icon and a chevron.

##### Variedades / VariedadesCompu

- A secondary "Sacar del stock" sits next to "+ Entrada de stock": a 44 px `#F2EEE6` pill.
- It wraps to its own row rather than shrinking text, and stays visible when the list is empty. It opens the form with no variety chosen.
- The list's own states are unchanged.

##### VariedadDetalle

- **The action**: "Sacar del stock", the same secondary pill. It goes on its own row under "+ Entrada" and "Nuevo precio" when three do not fit. It opens the form with this variety chosen.
- **"Stock y entradas"**: the last 3 movements, newest date first, then most recently recorded first.
  - A withdrawal row uses the entry-row layout: date and reason word on the left, this variety's "−N g" on the right.
  - When the withdrawal has pending cost, the row adds a second line, "Costo pendiente", with the clock.
  - A withdrawal that touches several varieties appears on each one's page with that variety's grams.
- The page's other states are unchanged.

##### Variety movements list ("Ver todas" of "Stock y entradas", new)

- **Built from**: the rows of `TodasLasEntradas` and the chrome of `VerTodas` ("‹" to the variety's page, no "+ Nueva venta").
- **Content**: one variety's entries and withdrawals.
- **States**: loading, filled, offline, error, and the stale-list notice.
- "Todas las entradas" is unchanged.

##### Inicio / InicioCompu / InicioOscuro

- In Semana and Mes, "Promoción $ X" and "Consumo propio $ X" use the existing line style, after Faltante de stock and before Gastos.
- Each line is left out, never drawn at $ 0, when the period has no withdrawal of that reason. Hoy is unchanged.
- "incluye costo pendiente" follows the conditional rule above.

##### Analisis / AnalisisCompu

- The two lines appear in the month header, the week and month rows and the totals since the first record, each with its own "y N g sin costo".
- The variety table, best week and best weekday are untouched.

##### Estados and toasts

`Estados` is unchanged. The new texts are the toasts "Retiro guardado" and "Retiro eliminado", with no figures.

#### Metrics

- **Record time (criterion 1)**:
  - From a phone viewport, a withdrawal is recorded in under 60 s.
  - The form opens in at most 2 taps from every tab root, on phone and computer, never more on the computer.
- **Stock and lots (criterion 2)**, 100% passing:
  - After a record, an edit and a delete, stock equals in minus out, to the hundredth.
  - Editing or deleting a withdrawal moves only that variety's costs.
  - Pending grams are never costed $0.
  - A withdrawal past zero stock is recorded and shows sold out.
- **Ganancia lines (criteria 3, 5)**, 100% passing, over a seeded history with:
  - a costed withdrawal of each reason;
  - a withdrawal with pending grams;
  - a negative adjustment;
  - a sale line with pending grams.

  Checks:
  - Inicio's Semana and Mes equal Análisis's row for the same week and month, with 0 differences.
  - Each line lowers Ganancia on its own date.
  - Neither line enters Costo de lo vendido or Faltante de stock.
  - A period with no withdrawal of a reason shows no line for it, and Hoy shows neither.
  - Each line's pending count counts only its own reason, and Ganancia's adds every pending gram behind it.
- **Not a sale, not a loss (criterion 4)**: 0 differences in:
  - sales total and count;
  - average sale;
  - Ventas;
  - every customer's balance and account;
  - Caja;
  - Faltante de stock;
  - every variety's margin and grams sold or gifted.
- **Stock side**:
  - Days left counts withdrawn grams.
  - A variety with only a withdrawal is not "sin entradas".
  - "Sin gramos desde" follows the withdrawal's date.
- **Validation matrix**: each case gives its own coded refusal, which never repeats the input:
  - grams at or below zero;
  - more than two decimals;
  - a future date;
  - a missing reason;
  - no lines;
  - a note over 200 characters;
  - another business's variety.
- **Isolation (criterion 6)**: every route is in the sweep and answers 404 or empty from another business.
- **No echo**:
  - No refusal or log line contains the note, grams or amounts.
  - After a front run, the console and browser storage hold none of them.
- **Front tests**:
  - the form chrome and the discard question;
  - the detail chrome;
  - both confirmations;
  - both toasts;
  - both "Eso ya no está." forms;
  - every inline refusal;
  - history rows told apart by word and sign;
  - the per-variety movements list;
  - no business-wide list.
- **Upkeep**:
  - A withdrawal that changes the catalog text edits the pinned message.
  - One that leaves the text unchanged sends nothing.
- **No curl**: the end-to-end front run:
  - records a withdrawal from Más and one from a variety's page;
  - edits and deletes one;
  - reads Promoción and Consumo propio on Inicio and Análisis.
- **Looks as designed**: `scripts/design/compare.sh` before and after, for the form, the detail, Más, Variedades, the variety's page, the movements list, Inicio and Análisis, on phone and computer, judged by eye.

#### Out of scope

- Who attended a party, or how many tried.
- Tying a promotion to later sales.
- Money spent on a party (that is an expense).
- A business-wide list of withdrawals.
- A per-variety breakdown of promotion or own-use cost.
- Planned or draft withdrawals.
- Splitting one withdrawal across two reasons.
- Live updates between partners.

#### Dependencies

The screens follow the new navigation (#58/#59) and the phone standard (#80). The backend and glossary can land first.

### Questions for the human

None.

### Observations on the design

- **Record a withdrawal, Path B (VariedadDetalle)**: opening the form from a variety's page takes 3 taps from a tab root, one more than the 2-tap budget. The budget still holds overall, because Más and Variedades each open the form in 2 taps.
- **Record a withdrawal, touch counts**: the flow counted Path A at 7 touches and Path C at 6. Both are 6 for one line, and the PRD uses the corrected count.
- **Record a withdrawal, variety gone while the form is open**: accounts.md's "Eso ya no está." rule closes the form and throws away everything typed. For a withdrawal with several lines, that is a costly loss.
- **Withdrawal detail, back**: the flow left out the fallback to Variedades when there is no history. The PRD and the screens design both state it.
- **Inicio, pending note**: the flow describes "incluye costo pendiente" as always shown. The PRD keeps the conditional rule: it shows only when that line, or Ganancia, has pending cost.
- **Sacar del stock form, keyboard**: the form does not use ADR-0028's visual-viewport pinning. Check by eye at 390 px with the keyboard open.
- **Sacar del stock form, footer cost**: "Costo que sale del stock" is something the PRD did not ask for. It should stay one compact line, and its figure must come from the API.
- **VariedadDetalle, actions**: the third action has no drawn layout. Check its own row at 390 px and at 130% text.
- **Stock y entradas → Ver todas**: the link must be re-pointed from TodasLasEntradas to the per-variety movements list in screens.json, navigation.md and the prototype.
- **Inicio**: the prototype draws no "Faltante de stock" line or pending note, so the placement of the new lines can only be judged against the text.
- **Stock y entradas, pending row**: the clock plus "Costo pendiente" on a history row is borrowed from the existing pending notes and has never been drawn on a row. Check it by eye.

## Interview: Tech Lead efímero→Tech Lead

### Round 1: questions

### Round 1

Failure I can already name: `build_draw_rows` in `app/stock/adapters/draw_rows.py` decides `kind` with a nested ternary. `is_shortfall = draw.sale_id is None`, then `"gifted"` if the pricing rule is GIFT, else `"sold"`. A withdrawal draw has no `sale_id` and no pricing rule. Added as a `Draw` without a new branch, it is classified as `"shortfall"`, so Promoción and Consumo propio grams would become stock shortfall and feed the cost-of-goods and margin figures. The PRD says they must never do that. Even with a new branch, `lots_and_draws` in `lot_order.py` accepts only `entries` and `sale_draws`, and `LotOrder.rank` knows only `STOCK_ENTRY_RANK` and `SALE_LINE_RANK`. A withdrawal's tie order against a sale line and a negative adjustment on the same date and instant is undefined today.

❓ **Q1** - **Where withdrawals live and how a new draw is told apart**: `app/stock/` already holds `Draw`, `SaleLineDraw`, `lots_and_draws`, `build_draw_rows` and `SqlDrawCosts`. Does the plan put withdrawals in `app/stock/` or in a new hexagon? How does a withdrawal become a `Draw` (a third source next to `SaleLineDraw`, with its own `rank`)? How does `DrawKind` gain `promotion` and `own_use` without a default branch? Stock's domain must not import `app.sales`, and sales must not import withdrawals.
